Engineering Department wonder generated files cover shared mechanics for all 54 generic wonders from `data/wonders.yaml` plus the 123 unique historical wonders from `data/unique_wonders.yaml`, with shared mechanical values in `data/wonder_final_buildings.yaml + data/wonder_generic_rituals.yaml + data/wonder_base_modifiers.yaml + data/wonder_site_rules.yaml`. The core generated set now includes triggers, scripted effects, building types, timed ritual burden and blessing static modifiers, unique ceremony static modifiers, generated country and location static display mirrors, style-2 ritual annex location modifiers, and `src_engineering_department/in_game/common/auto_modifiers/tv_engineering_department_wonder_mechanics_auto_modifiers.txt`. `tv_wonder_index_effects.txt` remains the generated global index: numeric wonder ids are canonical runtime ids, ritual branch ids reserve `wonder_id * 100 + style`, suitability row ids reserve `mechanic_id * 10 + row`, and startup/save-load hooks directly rebuild current maps without version-gated compatibility routers. `tv_wonder_initialize_existing_unique_wonders_effect` is the generated one-shot historical-start initializer for unique wonders whose data sets `initial_level > 0`; levels 1-5 are registered as saved priority expansion candidates without survey-result cache rows so Great Engineer proposal refresh can surface them before the random deck while still requiring survey before construction, and level 6 starts capped. `tv_wonder_module_effects.txt` uses captured helper/module/final `building_type` maps for construction, module materialization, final-state restoration, and saved priority resume/upgrade candidates. Final-building local effects are strict shared maps, national/base country effects are written into final building `modifier` blocks only for display, and actual national/base country effects come from generated Country Auto modifiers whose potential and scaling read the monthly-refreshed country map `tv_wonder_auto_level_by_wonder_id` instead of scanning owned locations. Non-numeric country effects such as privateer unlocks are emitted as hidden unscaled companion auto modifiers, and ritual country effects are granted through ritual static modifiers at completion. The action/concept/localization/GUI generators cover all 54 generic wonders and ids 101-223 unique wonders through the same generated path; localization mirrors wonder concept names/descriptions, base `AUTO_MODIFIER_NAME_tv_wonder_auto_*` keys, ritual static modifier keys, and generated display-only static mirror keys. `src_engineering_department/in_game/gui/location_window.gui` projects numeric id/level/ritual-style values into compact and tooltip slots, with fixed unique sites shown at level 0 when absent at start, historical-start unique wonders shown from stored `initial_level`, and generic wonders shown only after a final wonder building level exists.

Location-window tooltip illustrations and Engineering Department overview previews use full 3:2 `tv_wonder_display_full_image_<id>` route concepts; compact location cards keep using cropped `tv_wonder_display_image_<id>` route concepts where the wide crop is intended.

Governor's House War Tent file notes: `src/in_game/common/scripted_effects/tv_govhouse_effects.txt` contains the tactic, war-plan progress, modifier refresh, war-entry, war-end, monthly War Tent effects, and plan-activation event routing; `src/in_game/common/casus_belli/tv_govhouse_casus_belli.txt` defines the event-granted War Plan CB that uses the vanilla `superiority_rival` wargoal; `src/in_game/common/generic_actions/tv_govhouse_actions.txt` contains the offensive/defensive plan, abort, and change-target actions; `src/in_game/common/on_action/tv_govhouse_war_tent_on_action.txt` defines the war declaration, war join, and war-ending callback bodies registered by `tv_pulse_bridges.txt`; `src/in_game/events/tv_govhouse_events.txt` contains the tactic approval event, offensive/defensive plan activation events, and the completed offensive-plan CB event; `src/in_game/gui/panels/organization/tv_govhouse.gui` now separates the overview, Grand General tab, and War Tent custom tab.

### Main Menu Graphics

`src/main_menu/common/modifier_icons/` contains TV modifier type icon mappings. `towards_victory_modifier_icons.txt` maps custom price, goods, and IO special-status modifier types to close vanilla `gfx/interface/icons/modifier_types/*.dds` assets, so the engine can resolve their UI icons without custom DDS files.

`src/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/international_organizations/` contains the six TV IO `GetIcon` assets:
`tv_govhouse.dds`, `tv_diplomatic_alliance.dds`, `tv_arts_exhibition.dds`, `tv_academy_of_sciences.dds`, `tv_engineering_department.dds`, and `tv_trade_league.dds`.
`tv_trade_league.dds` reuses the Trade Victory `gfx/interface/icons/flat_icons/trade.dds` asset under the IO type key. These assets keep shared IO title/list/tooltip UI from falling back to the generic international organization icon.

`src/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/laws/` contains seven Academy law icon assets copied from vanilla advance icons and six Governor's House law icon assets copied from related vanilla law icons for the original Governor's House policy groups.

`src_engineering_department/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/towards_victory/wonders/` contains the Engineering Department wonder image assets. Besides per-wonder DDS files, the fixed event-stage asset set is `tv_wonder_construction.dds`, `tv_wonder_foundation.dds`, `tv_wonder_body.dds`, `tv_wonder_function.dds`, `tv_wonder_decoration.dds`, and `tv_wonder_complete.dds`. The Engineering Department overview tab displays the matching full 3:2 image for the current Great Engineer proposal before lock-in, then keeps displaying the locked target wonder during later construction stages. The illustration area is hidden only when there is no proposal and no locked target wonder. Location-window compact images resolve through `GetConceptTexture(Concatenate('tv_wonder_display_image_', id))` cropped route concepts, while Engineering Department overview previews and location-window tooltip illustrations resolve through `GetConceptTexture(Concatenate('tv_wonder_display_full_image_', id))` full 3:2 route concepts; generated textures point explicitly at this `gfx/interface/icons/` directory.

`src_engineering_department/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/trade_goods/` and `src_engineering_department/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/trade_goods/illustrations/` currently hold the `tv_wonder_materials` trade-good icon pair. `src_engineering_department/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/buildings/` contains the generated `tv_wonder_labor_camp.dds` Wonder Worksite building icon, named after the building type so vanilla `GetBuildingIcon` resolution applies it automatically; the `wonder_building_icons` DDS batch is configured to populate the same directory with one 128x128 icon per generic or unique final wonder building. `src/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/situations/tv_victory_situation.dds` is the main Towards Victory situation icon, and `src/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/towards_victory/victory_paths/` holds the six generated route identity icons named `tv_victory_{path}.dds` used by the generated victory situation GUI. `src/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/towards_victory/victory_rewards/` holds generated 128x128 mipmapped victory reward icons named `tv_victory_{path}_template.dds` and `tv_victory_{path}_m{milestone}_reward_{choice}.dds`, which are wired into the victory reward buttons. `src/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/towards_victory/victory_trees/` holds six 2048x1152 DXT1 victory reward-tree background assets named `tv_victory_{path}_tree.dds`; the generated victory situation GUI renders each path's tree background as a stretched `-1 220` banner at the top of that path's content container, directly above the path overview card. The repository includes a reusable DDS icon generation workflow in `scripts/generate_dds_icon.py` plus `generate_dds_icon_config.json`: it selects one generation target (`trade_good_icon`, `trade_good_illustration`, `building_icon`, `victory_situation_icon`, `victory_path_icon`, `victory_reward_icon`, or `victory_tree_background`) or a batch mode (`victory_path_icons`, `victory_reward_icons`, `victory_tree_backgrounds`, or `wonder_building_icons`), can apply target-specific asset names and image prompt overrides, refines a short natural-language request into a target-aware production prompt for API targets, uploads that target's same-type style reference DDS/PNG files, then writes configured DDS output with enforced dimensions, file-size limits, optional mipmaps, and a circular inner-crop pass with transparent outside pixels plus feathered alpha at the edge for icon targets. It skips generation when the final DDS exists and `output.overwrite` is false, can re-convert an existing generated PNG into the selected single DDS target with `--convert-existing-png`, and target configs may enable `local_template` rendering for deterministic source-DDS transformations such as the Wonder Worksite icon based on vanilla `construction_center.dds`. The `victory_path_icons` batch creates one situation icon plus six route icons; the `victory_reward_icons` batch first generates six route templates, then uses each template as the style reference for that route's fifteen route/milestone/reward-option icons; the `victory_tree_backgrounds` batch creates six route-specific 16:9 backgrounds with a blurred route backdrop and an explicit, button-ready foreground tree topology; and the `wonder_building_icons` batch reads `data/wonders.yaml`, `data/unique_wonders.yaml`, `data/wonder_image_prompts.yaml`, and `data/wonder_localization.yaml` through the wonder-mechanics loader to create 162 generic final-building icon tasks plus 123 unique inaugurated-building icon tasks in vanilla building-icon style. `scripts_engineering_department/generate_wonder_image.py` plus `scripts_engineering_department/generate_wonder_image_config.json` is the batch wonder-image helper: it prepends the six fixed stage tasks from `fixed_wonder_stage_image_prompts`, then reads generic prompts from `data/wonder_image_prompts.yaml` and unique prompts from `data/unique_wonders.yaml`, writes PNG metadata under `data/generated_wonders/`, writes full single-level DDS assets under `src_engineering_department/main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/towards_victory/wonders/`, writes parallel 27:11 event-image DDS siblings named `{stem}_cropped.dds` for PNG-backed tasks, can rebuild existing wonder DDS files with `--convert-existing-assets` without calling the image API, applies persisted PNG crop records from `data/wonder_image_crops.json` to the cropped siblings when they exist, and keeps the JSON config limited to API/runtime selection, per-task overrides, and overwrite defaults. `scripts_engineering_department/wonder_image_cropper.py` is the Engineering Department browser crop tool for those records: it serves a local standard-library web UI, lets the user drag a fixed-ratio crop frame over generated wonder PNGs, save with Enter or the Save and Next button, reload saved crop rectangles by image stem, remove a crop record, and apply the current crop data to rebuild only the cropped DDS siblings without overwriting the full assets.

`unique_wonders_site/` is a root-level static, read-only atlas for the 54 generic Engineering Department wonders and 123 unique historical wonders. It reuses the map tiles and `locations_index.json` from `reference_mods/national_destinies_site/dist`, generates `dist/data/unique_wonders.json` from `data/unique_wonders.yaml`, `data/wonders.yaml`, `data/wonder_final_buildings.yaml + data/wonder_generic_rituals.yaml + data/wonder_base_modifiers.yaml + data/wonder_site_rules.yaml`, `data/wonder_localization.yaml`, and `data/index/{modifier,trigger}_localization.json`, and presents a left-side switch between Generic Wonders and Unique Wonders. Unique wonders render searchable map pins grouped by location; generic wonders have no map pins and open their right-side detail panel only from the left-side generic wonder list. Both modes show localized names/descriptions, localized construction-requirement rows parsed from site triggers, and localized modifier/effect rows matching the wonder editor's modifier/reward display style. Unique detail data also exposes/searches the game-start state as either start level 1 or not present at start.

## AI Workflow Knowledge

The currently supported release localization set is English and Simplified Chinese. German localization remains future work: `scripts/validate.py` must not require German key completeness or static-modifier names merely because a partial German directory exists.

AI workflow state is maintained as project source, not by manual human follow-up. `scripts/ai_context.py` builds the task-scoped context from changed or explicit files, generated-file metadata, `docs/knowledge/anti_patterns.yaml`, short domain risk cards in `docs/knowledge/risk_cards/`, and content markers for `variable_map` / `global_variable_map` / `local_variable_map` usage. Current registered risk cards: `events.md`, `generic_actions.md`, `gui.md`, `international_organizations.md`, `on_action.md`, `wonders.md`, `philosophy_debate.md`, and `trade_league.md`; event files route to the events card because option tooltips can pre-evaluate option effect chains, including `hidden_effect`, while IO definitions, IO laws, and country interactions that find or mutate TV IOs route to the international-organization card because IO scopes are high risk. The `wonders`, `philosophy_debate`, and `trade_league` domains are not isolated in their own directory — their files are named-but-interspersed across `common/scripted_effects`, `static_modifiers`, `building_types`, `generic_actions`, and `gui` — so `ai_context.py` routes them by filename substring (`FILENAME_DOMAIN_RULES`) instead of path prefix: `wonder`/`engineering_department` filenames route to `wonders.md`, `philosophy_debate`/`world_debate`/`academy_debate` filenames route to `philosophy_debate.md`, and `trade_league`/`trade_chain` filenames route to `trade_league.md`. Files containing variable-map content route to the local Variable maps documentation and prioritize the `variable_map_scope_link_used_direct_rhs` anti-pattern because direct quoted map lookups are unsafe as RHS/effect parameters. Anti-pattern entries use `detectability` to distinguish safe regex lint (`lint`), parser/semantic rules (`needs_parser`), and AI-facing warnings (`advisory`), and may name a semantic `validator` implemented in `scripts/validate.py`. `scripts/validate.py` enforces safe lint rules, emits generic-action pre-evaluation and interaction-target chooser warnings, rejects visible generic-action calls to registered hidden-only cleanup helpers, emits IO policy AI-scope warnings/errors, fails duplicate main-menu localization keys within the same language pack, checks that every static modifier has `STATIC_MODIFIER_NAME_<id>` localization in every supported main-menu language, checks that every `modifier_type_definitions` entry has `MODIFIER_TYPE_NAME_<id>`/`MODIFIER_TYPE_DESC_<id>` localization plus a `modifier_icons` entry, checks that every `auto_modifiers` entry has `AUTO_MODIFIER_NAME_<id>` localization, checks that every `prices` entry has a bare `<id>` localization key, rejects dotted event references whose numeric event ID is `>= 10000`, rejects direct `effect = { ... }` child blocks inside event options, flags any `construct_building` call that sets `cost_multiplier` without a sibling `cost_multiplier_reason`, checks `# @validate_local_variable_cleanup` blocks for top-level `remove_local_variable` cleanup after local scratch assignments, walks `monthly_country_pulse` registry callbacks and TV helper calls to require the configured one-day event delay, checks that risk cards remain registered in `ai_context.py`, checks that each TV IO type has a matching `main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/international_organizations/<io_type>.dds` GetIcon asset, rejects TV IO `monthly_effect` blocks, checks that generated main and M&T compatibility `location_window.gui` outputs match their current generators/reference inputs, and checks that TV does not stack a second direct `effect` onto hardcoded vanilla on_action hooks such as `on_annex` or `on_winning_war`. Its `auto_modifiers`/`static_modifiers` modifier-name check tracks brace depth so operator keywords nested inside a `scales_with = { ... }`/`limit = { ... }` sub-block (`value`, `add`, `subtract`, `multiply`, `divide`, `min`, `max`) are never mistaken for top-level modifier names; a `<price_name>_cost_modifier` tag still needs its own explicit `modifier_type_definitions` entry like any other custom modifier (it is not engine-auto-derived). `scripts/test_lint_rules.py` runs fixture tests from `tests/fixtures/anti_patterns/<rule_id>/` for selected lint regexes and registered semantic validators.

All AI workflow command examples distinguish normal user terminals from managed sandboxes. In Codex, Claude subagents, or other managed sandboxes, project Python scripts are run with `C:\Users\Hades\anaconda3\envs\eu5\python.exe scripts\...`; agents must not run or retry `conda run -n eu5` there because the read-only Anaconda base metadata path can make it hang. The `conda run --no-capture-output -n eu5 python ...` form is reserved for normal user terminals and generated `Regen:` documentation lines. `scripts/ai_context.py` prints managed-sandbox validation suggestions with the direct interpreter form.

For Engineering Department wonders, remember the noncapital building rule: EU5 building `modifier` / `raw_modifier` are local, while `capital_country_modifier` only applies country effects if the building is in the capital. Since wonders can be outside the capital, final wonder buildings keep base national/global effects in their `modifier` block only as display data; the real base country effects are generated as level-gated Country Auto modifiers, while real ritual country effects are applied as static modifiers when the selected ritual completes.

Wonder Construction random events are data-driven from `data/wonder_construction_events.yaml`. The generated outputs are `src_engineering_department/in_game/events/tv_wonder_construction_events.txt`, `src_engineering_department/in_game/common/scripted_effects/tv_wonder_construction_event_effects.txt`, `src_engineering_department/in_game/common/scripted_triggers/tv_wonder_construction_event_triggers.txt`, and the matching English/Simplified Chinese localization files. All generated construction-random events use the static `tv_wonder_construction.dds` event image. Hand-maintained Engineering Department wonder events also default to `tv_wonder_construction.dds`, except module synthesis uses `tv_wonder_complete.dds`, body/function/decoration module-complete split events use `tv_wonder_body.dds`, `tv_wonder_function.dds`, and `tv_wonder_decoration.dds`, and finalization/world-news events use the completed wonder's `{wonder_image_name}_cropped.dds` event-image sibling. Re-run all five `gen_tv_wonder_construction_events*` generators after changing token values, event categories, weights, eligibility rules, or flavor text.

## Script Reference

| Script | Input(s) | Output(s) | When to run |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/update_mod_version.py` | Current local date, or `--date YYYY-MM-DD` | Date-stamped `version` fields in all six first-party metadata files plus matching Great Project dependency constraints | Automatically at the start of `build.bat`; use `--check` to verify without writing |
| `scripts/validate.py --changed` | src/ + src_engineering_department/ + src_court_positions/ mod files + data/generated_files.yaml + data/validation_baseline.yaml + vanilla-copy integrity checks + IO monthly_effect ban + location_window generated-freshness checks + localization duplicate/static-modifier-name scans | Console/JSON report (exit 0/1); new unbaselined warnings fail validation | Before launching game |
| `scripts/ai_context.py --changed/--files` | git changed files or explicit paths + data/generated_files.yaml + risk cards + anti_patterns.yaml + variable-map content markers | Compact AI task context, required risk-card list, required variable-map reads, and managed-sandbox validation commands using the direct `eu5` interpreter | At AI session start or before editing a task domain |
| `scripts/gen_brief.py` | anti_patterns.yaml + valid_enums.yaml + PROJECT_OVERVIEW.md | docs/knowledge/BRIEF.md | After editing any knowledge YAML |
| `scripts/test_lint_rules.py` | docs/knowledge/anti_patterns.yaml + tests/fixtures/anti_patterns/ | Fixture test report for anti-pattern lint regexes and registered validators | After adding or changing `detectability: lint` rules |
| `scripts/gen_index.py` | reference_game_files + src/ + src_engineering_department/ + src_court_positions/ | data/index/*.txt + data/index/modifier_localization.json + data/index/trigger_localization.json | Run by gen_brief.py automatically |
| `scripts_engineering_department/gen_wonder_editor_catalog.py` | reference_game_files/game/in_game/events + reference_game_files/game/main_menu/common/static_modifiers + reference_game_files/game/main_menu/common/modifier_type_definitions | data/wonder_editor_catalog.yaml | After updating vanilla reference files; refreshes wonder-editor country/local options from vanilla event-applied static modifiers and Style 3 instant reward options |

Generated files that copy vanilla sources preserve the copied vanilla content verbatim except for explicit TV insertion points. `gen_character_title.py` and `gen_messagetypes.py` write root-specific full copies: standalone roots carry only their own insertions, while the main TV root carries the TV + Engineering Department union and must be manually sorted after Engineering Department when both are enabled. `check_vanilla_copy_integrity()` validates the copied base and each root's expected singleton coverage. The on_action bridge registry generator emits one TV-owned bridge file per applicable root, containing shared parent hooks such as `monthly_country_pulse`, `yearly_country_pulse`, `on_character_death`, `on_ruler_death`, startup/load hooks, war hooks, subject hooks, ownership hooks, institution-embraced hooks, capital-moved hooks, and CMF callbacks. Those blocks register only TV-owned callbacks through `on_actions = { ... }` and let vanilla or feature files provide the rest of each hook body. The bridge file must not use vanilla filenames such as `country_monthly.txt`, because those collide with vanilla files and can replace rather than extend them. Feature on_action files should keep only named `tv_*` callback bodies; parent hook bridge registration belongs in `data/pulse_registry.yaml` and generated `tv_*_pulse_bridges.txt`. The intended singleton insertions are TV character-title entries from `data/io_leaders.yaml` and per-root generic-action message types from `scripts/gen_messagetypes.py`; never hand-edit any root's generated full copy. `data/pulse_registry.yaml` owns the bridge on_action names and `settings.monthly_country_pulse_event_delay_days`, currently 1; any TV event whose conditions are checked by a `monthly_country_pulse` callback must use `days = 1` on direct `trigger_event_*` calls or `delay = { days = 1 }` before native `events` / `random_events` ids.

## Manual UI Notes

- `src_engineering_department/in_game/gui/location_window.gui` is generated from vanilla plus TV insertions. The location-scene wonder card uses fixed generated templates: three compact slots and five hover-tooltip slots, both filled by unique wonders first and generic wonders second, with one overflow line in the hover tooltip for any additional wonders beyond five. Slot state stores numeric ids, level, and ritual style only. Unique historical sites display non-starting wonders at level 0 before construction and game-start historical wonders at stored level 1 after startup/load initialization; level 0 tooltip rows show the name/image and `TV_LOCATION_WONDER_NO_EFFECT` instead of trying to route to a non-existent level-0 modifier or ritual effect. GUI names use dynamic clickable concept links built from id-based `tv_wonder_display_<id>` route concepts; compact-card images use cropped `tv_wonder_display_image_<id>` route concepts, tooltip illustrations use full 3:2 `tv_wonder_display_full_image_<id>` route concepts shared with Engineering Department overview previews, country/local effect columns use dynamic `ShowModifierEffect` keys backed by generated static mirrors and unreachable script references, and ritual effect lists are recovered through `tv_wonder_display_<id>_ritual_<style>_location_tooltip_effect` route names; both the ritual effect list and its mutually-exclusive "no effect" fallback (a plain `text_single`, not `TooltipTextBlock`) are sized to match the country/local modifier columns (13pt text, 22px rows) while keeping the full effect-column width. Each expanded tooltip row also opens with an untitled requirements block above the country/local modifier columns (no separate title text, since the location the panel is already anchored to makes the "construction conditions" framing self-evident), showing the location's own base site rule requirements via `ShowTriggerConditionsForScope` against a generated `tv_wonder_display_<id>_base_site_rules_trigger` alias, evaluated directly in the location's own scope (no `any_owned_location` wrapper, since the GUI root is already the location). That alias re-embeds the wonder's site-rule condition list directly rather than delegating to the real gameplay trigger `tv_wonder_location_meets_<key>_base_site_rules_trigger`, so unique wonders' `this = location:<site>` guard (still required on the real trigger for construction eligibility) is omitted from the display — the tooltip is already showing that exact location, so the row would be redundant. The GUI does not use `GetFlagName` or runtime raw-DDS path concatenation.
- `submods/tv_meiou_and_taxes_compat/in_game/gui/location_window.gui` is generated from the MEIOU and Taxes `reference_mods/3735059838` location window plus the same TV wonder tooltip and scene-overlay renderer. The submod contains no copied TV or M&T assets/localization/common scripts; its metadata depends on `hades.towards_victory.great_project` and `meiou_and_taxes`, and `submods/README.md` defines the compatibility-submod maintenance rules.
- `submods/tv_standard_of_living_compat/in_game/gui/location_window.gui` is generated the same way from the Standard of Living `reference_mods/3698931463` location window; its metadata depends on `hades.towards_victory.great_project` and `hades.sol`.
- `src_engineering_department/in_game/gui/encyclopedia_lateralview.gui` is a hand-authored full override of vanilla's Europedia (Encyclopedia) panel adding a "Towards Victory" tab, toggled by a `tv_encyclopedia_active` `GetVariableSystem` variable (EU5 has no native, data-driven way to register a new Encyclopedia sidebar page/category — see `docs/knowledge/risk_cards/europedia.md`). Every vanilla sidebar page button also clears the toggle so selecting a real page exits the tab. Inside the tab, four filter buttons (`tv_encyclopedia_filter`: all/mechanics/generic/unique) gate a generated card list — one card per Engineering Department mechanic concept, generic wonder archetype, and unique wonder (192 total), each reusing an existing `game_concept_<id>`/`_desc` localization pair rather than new prose. The card list is generated by `scripts_engineering_department/in_game/gui/gen_tv_encyclopedia_wonders_cards_gui.py` and spliced into the shell by `scripts_engineering_department/in_game/gui/merge_tv_encyclopedia_wonders_cards_gui.py` via a `# BEGIN/END GENERATED TV_ENCYCLOPEDIA_CARDS` marker pair; the shell file itself is hand-maintained, matching the `tv_engineering_department.gui` precedent.
- `submods/tv_prosper_or_perish_compat/in_game/gui/encyclopedia_lateralview.gui` is generated by `scripts/compat/gen_tv_prosper_or_perish_encyclopedia_lateralview.py` from TV's own `encyclopedia_lateralview.gui` plus Prosper or Perish's (`reference_mods/3613232232`) — the same released mod cited as the tab-toggle pattern's precedent in `docs/knowledge/risk_cards/europedia.md`, and it also fully overrides this file with its own tab (`pp_encyclopedia_active`/`pp_filter`). Since both mods patch the same shared vanilla nav buttons (not just add self-contained content), the generator rewrites both tabs onto one shared toggle variable, `tveu_compat_encyclopedia_active`, with distinct string values (`'tv'`/`'pp'`) instead of two independent booleans — this lets every vanilla nav button use a single `Clear(...)` call and a single `Not(GetVariableSystem.Exists(...))` mutual-exclusion check rather than needing unverified multi-effect `onclick` chaining. Its metadata depends on `hades.towards_victory.great_project` only, since Prosper or Perish declares no internal mod id of its own to depend on formally.
- `src/in_game/gui/panels/organization/tv_arts_exhibition.gui` displays the Arts Exhibition touring action, current dispatched artist count, and all currently dispatched artists from `tv_dispatched_artists_list` as `io_character_card` artist rows in the overview tab. The header shows the variable-backed Chief Artist portrait/name when appointed; if vacant, the portrait area remains clickable to appoint a Chief Artist, and if occupied, two small Replace/Dismiss buttons sit in the portrait area's upper-right corner. A separate monthly International Influence contribution readout remains in the header. The resolutions tab is renamed to Arts Exchange and contains the exchange timer, participant list, and exchange action cards. The vanilla Laws tab is available for Arts Exhibition legal policy votes. Its custom tab tracks the persistent local exhibition: owned artworks, committed exhibits, months open, rating, enthusiasm progress bar, and local exhibition actions.
- `src/in_game/gui/panels/organization/tv_govhouse.gui` keeps a fixed semi-transparent Grand General Actions card in the header, showing the commanded army link, wartime planning, annexation assistance, inspections, or no-appointment fallback; keeps governor region management in the overview, including an Auto-Assign Best Governors button and adjacent `button_checkbox_auto` automation checkbutton above the region list; shows each assigned regional governor's current autonomous task and its effect below the character card; uses the vanilla IO parliament tab for Governor's House legal policy votes with Diplomatic Alliance-style support / not-voted / opposition vote cards; moves Grand General portrait/balance/training controls and active training progress to the resolutions tab; and uses the custom tab for War Tent tactics, war-plan status, and plan action buttons.
- `src_engineering_department/in_game/gui/panels/organization/tv_engineering_department.gui` hides the Members tab; the header shows the variable-backed Great Engineer portrait/name when appointed, keeps the vacant portrait area clickable for appointment, places Replace/Dismiss controls in the portrait area's upper-right corner, and includes a right-aligned Current Guidance card that changes its game-concept-linked instruction across appointment, proposal, debate, survey, site confirmation, construction, ceremony, and finalization states. The overview tab contains active Conception and Debate controls and displays the current proposal or locked target wonder's illustration below the proposal/action controls whenever one exists, including unique wonder images when their DDS assets exist; the illustration overlays a top location/type card, and Domestic Support is hidden until a wonder has been locked. The resolutions tab is Survey and Logistics: it contains locked-stage info cards before prerequisites, the active survey card, survey result readouts, and after site confirmation an Organization/Logistics card split into Organization and Logistics sections, each with explanatory text, an icon/progress row, and a native Production filter button for Wonder Worksites or Wonder Material Workshops; the survey card begins with Suitability Knowledge, showing the current wonder's location trigger conditions and placeholder/revealed suitability rows, with revealed rows displaying unknown/current actual bonus against the maximum bonus. The custom tab is Construction and Ceremony: it contains locked-stage info cards, construction-part buttons, active-part progress, the completion action, toggle-style ceremony selection for generic wonders, and two selected-branch-only detail panels for ritual requirements and ritual effects. For the 121 shared unique ceremonies, construction completion selects style 1, refreshes the selected ritual cache, and starts the ritual at stage 0; no ready card or shared Hold Ceremony button is shown, and the first stage event arrives after three monthly pulses. Pharos and Hagia Sophia retain their dedicated manual buttons. Generic branches show their requirement and reward through `ShowScriptedEffectForScope` effect lists. Unique wonders skip style selection because their single ritual branch is auto-selected and locked at construction completion; their requirement/effect panels route through the selected ritual's real static modifier and reward tooltip effects rather than final-building Country Auto mirrors.
- `src/in_game/gui/panels/organization/tv_academy_of_sciences.gui` is generated from `data/locked_advances.yaml` and `data/philosophy_debates.yaml`. Its header shows the variable-backed Chief Scientist portrait/name when appointed, keeps the vacant portrait area clickable for appointment, places Replace/Dismiss controls in the portrait area's upper-right corner, and includes a right-aligned Inspiration card that shows only the Chief Scientist's current activity plus `tv_academy_philosophy_inspiration` progress. Its overview slot hosts the Philosophy debate display: an untitled current-node line, a five-node timeline with History/Local/World icons and year labels, and a current issue card that switches between a historical roundtable, a local six-seat roundtable with Crown chair and five generated debate seats showing group icons and support/oppose/neutral stance markers, or a live 50-seat world debate grid with country flags and green/red/yellow support/oppose/neutral tinting. Local progress reads the Academy IO variable `tv_academy_philosophy_debate_position`; world debate rows read mirrored world Situation variables for current issue, seat stance, strength, and debate progress, and the old six-circle/action-card UI is not present. The resolutions slot is renamed Concentrated Research and opens with a collapsible "Potential Projects" card (`card_expandable`) listing all 31 Academy target advances as untitled rows (age icon, "Age N" label, colored target name, researched/not-researched status), each row's visibility gated by `tv_research_open_age_ceiling` compared to that advance's age — deliberately wider than the actual random-selection pool since it also shows already-researched advances in an open age band. Below that card the tab stages the Research Mechanism UI: with no target it shows only an instruction card; after target approval it shows a centered Research Target card plus A/B/C preparation progress and the B requirements list; once A/B/C are complete it replaces preparation progress with a completion info card and shows the Concentrated Research progress card below, followed by research action buttons. The Medicine tab contains treatment and meditation progress/actions.
- `src/in_game/gui/panels/situation/tv_academy_world_debate_situation.gui` is generated from `data/philosophy_debates.yaml` and displays the same world debate content directly from the active Situation scope: issue title, 50 flag seats with stance tinting, support strength, and debate progress.
- `src/in_game/gui/panels/organization/tv_trade_league.gui` is generated from `data/trade_league_goods.yaml` and `data/trade_chain.yaml`. Its header shows the variable-backed Grand Merchant portrait/name when appointed, keeps the vacant portrait area clickable for appointment, places Replace/Dismiss controls in the portrait area's upper-right corner, and adds a compact action card that displays law changes, intelligence-network expansion, or the assist-caravans idle fallback. The assist-caravans row also shows its +10% merchant maintenance efficiency effect from `tv_trade_league_assist_caravans_modifier`; Trade Chain no longer occupies the Grand Merchant, and promote-monopoly has no implemented state and is not displayed. Its overview tab contains Trade League invitation/expulsion controls plus the Trade Chain UI: a strength card with aligned length/flow/strength values, weak/medium/strong tier label, and one dynamic `ShowModifierEffect(Concatenate('tv_trade_chain_strength_display_', ...))` display row, followed by ordered market cards with slot 1 fixed to the capital market and one add-node card while count is below 20. Chain cards use 72px piechart/add-circle left glyphs, market owner / own capacity / trade advantage / trade profit columns, 8px spacing, and generated progress-bar backgrounds aligned to the pie/add centers. The vanilla Laws tab is available as Trade Laws for the five three-level Trade League law groups. The Resolutions tab is Commercial Intelligence: once a Grand Merchant is appointed, all members can page through fixed projected rows for global markets, league total trade advantage, and network strength; selecting a market opens a detail card with the top three merchant-power countries, network strength, a 0-100 progress bar, and leader-only Build/Cancel Network controls. Intelligence network state is stored on the Trade League leader by market slot; the active market gains Grand Merchant DIP / 100 strength each month, inactive markets lose 1 strength, and each full strength point is synchronized as keyed permanent `tv_trade_intelligence_network` merchant power for current members in that market. The custom tab is Monopolies: it remains locked until a Grand Merchant is appointed, then shows one rectangular card per unlocked monopoly slot, with 1 slot by default and 2 slots at 10 members. Slot actions refresh the player's slot and `tv_trade_selected_*` projection variables from the leader-owned monopoly state, so members can browse independently. Selecting a filled slot opens one projected monopoly detail card and one leader-only fixed action card for virtual demand, virtual production, and embargo controls.

## Known Anti-Patterns

| Detectability | Category | Scope | Bad Form | Correct Form | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| advisory | workflow | any | Adding or preserving compatibility layers, legacy save migrations, alias variables, wrapper launchers, duplicate old/new paths, or defensive fallback branches for a previous internal implementation | Treat this unreleased project as mutable. Date-based YYMMDD versions identify builds; they do not create compatibility contracts. Update all callers, generators, data, and docs to the current architecture, delete stale branches/state, and prefer direct efficient refactors over compatibility shims. Only add compatibility when the user explicitly requests it for a named external consumer. | There are no shipped saves or public APIs to protect. Compatibility code creates hot-path cost, hides call-order bugs, and teaches future agents to preserve obsolete structures. |
| advisory | workflow | any | Running or retrying `conda run -n eu5` from Codex, Claude subagents, or another managed sandbox | Run project scripts with the direct environment interpreter: `C:\Users\Hades\anaconda3\envs\eu5\python.exe scripts\<script>.py ...`. Reserve `conda run --no-capture-output -n eu5 python ...` for normal user terminals only. | Managed sandboxes can have read-only access to `C:\Users\Hades\anaconda3\conda-meta\history`, causing `conda run` to hang before the agent reaches validation or context output. |
| lint | script | any | Copying an existing vanilla trigger-localization key, scripted effect, or building type into an override file with the original bare top-level key | Prefix the patch with the narrowest correct directive: use `INJECT:<id>` when only adding a field that the vanilla definition does not contain (for example `INJECT:university = { on_construction_ended = { ... } }`); use `REPLACE:<id>` only when altering or replacing existing fields, preserving the complete original block plus the intended modification. This applies only to base-game vanilla identifiers guaranteed to exist regardless of load order — for a TV-namespaced key that only a non-guaranteed mod (e.g. the main `src/` mod) defines, see replace_prefix_requires_guaranteed_base_entry instead; REPLACE:-sharing that kind of key breaks whenever the defining mod isn't loaded. | EU5 does not infer a patch merely because a mod repeats an existing non-event database key. A bare later block is rejected as a duplicate; use INJECT for a new child field and REPLACE only when an existing definition must change. 2026-08-02: removed TV_HAS_VARIABLE_NOT_SET_TT from this pattern's example list after discovering it was the wrong fix for that specific key — see replace_prefix_requires_guaranteed_base_entry. |
| advisory | script | any | Using REPLACE:<id> in a standalone-loadable mod (e.g. src_engineering_department/, src_court_positions/) for a key that only a DIFFERENT, non-guaranteed mod (e.g. the main src/ mod) defines. | REPLACE:<id> requires <id> to already exist in the database at load time. A base-game vanilla identifier is always safe to REPLACE: regardless of mod combination. A key owned by another TV mod is safe to REPLACE: only if that mod is a guaranteed hard dependency. If the current mod must also work standalone (or with any subset of the other TV mods) and the key is not guaranteed present, give this mod its OWN uniquely-namespaced key instead of trying to share/override the other mod's key — the same 'narrow to your own namespace' resolution already used for Court Positions' message types (see PROJECT_OVERVIEW.md). | 2026-08-02: src_engineering_department/in_game/common/trigger_localization/tv_engineering_department_triggers.txt declared REPLACE:TV_HAS_VARIABLE_NOT_SET_TT, assuming the main src/ mod's bare TV_HAS_VARIABLE_NOT_SET_TT definition (src/in_game/common/trigger_localization/towards_victory_triggers.txt) had already loaded. This is true only when both mods run together — CLAUDE.md's own Project Identity section states Engineering Department must be fully playable standalone, and in that configuration the main mod's copy never loads, so REPLACE: has nothing to replace and errors. This was the ORIGINAL motivation for anti_patterns rule same_id_database_override_missing_replace_prefix's TV_HAS_VARIABLE_NOT_SET_TT example (added 2026-07-28, presumably tested only in the combined-mods configuration where REPLACE: happened to work), so that rule's guidance was itself incomplete for standalone-capable mods. Fixed by renaming the Engineering Department mod's copy to TV_ENGINEERING_DEPARTMENT_HAS_VARIABLE_NOT_SET_TT (bare declaration, no REPLACE: needed, all 3 internal custom_tooltip references and the EN/ZH game_concepts loc key renamed to match) rather than trying to share the main mod's key at all — eliminates the collision instead of resolving it via load-order-dependent REPLACE:. The main src/ mod's own TV_HAS_VARIABLE_NOT_SET_TT (used by ~50 unrelated references across src/) was left untouched. |
| advisory | building | any | Copying a vanilla building's `unique_production_methods` child ID unchanged into a `REPLACE:<building>` override | Keep the complete production-method definition, but rename each copied child to a unique `tv_`-prefixed ID (for example `university_maintenance` to `tv_university_maintenance`). | REPLACE swaps the building definition but does not replace a nested unique production method's global registration; retaining the vanilla child ID logs a duplicate-production-method error and prevents the override from loading cleanly. |
| advisory | event | any | Using REPLACE: or INJECT: for an individual vanilla event, or copying that event into a mod event file which loads after its base-game source file | Create a new event file whose filename sorts before the relevant base file (for example 0000_modded_events.txt), repeat the vanilla namespace, and copy only each modified event under its bare ID. Preserve the complete event block. If it calls a helper defined in the base event file, inline that helper's contents in the event or copy the helper under a new mod-specific name. The resulting duplicate-event-ID log entry is expected and harmless. | The event manager applies the earlier duplicate definition; event-specific REPLACE: and INJECT: are not a valid replacement mechanism. |
| lint | scope | country | Using any_ally/every_ally/random_ally/ordered_ally as if vanilla registered an active ally scripted list | Iterate known countries and filter the alliance relation explicitly: effects use every_known_country = { limit = { is_allied_with = { target = scope:saved_owner } } ... }; triggers use any_known_country = { is_allied_with = { target = root } ... }. | A commented example in common/scripted_lists is documentation or disabled content, not a registered runtime iterator. Emitting every_ally logged Unknown effect; emitting any_ally logged Unknown trigger type. |
| lint | script | any | Using effect control-flow blocks if/else_if/else inside common/scripted_triggers | Use trigger-native logic. For mutually exclusive value dispatch, use switch = { trigger = var:X <value> = { <trigger body> } }; otherwise express the condition with AND/OR/NOT. Keep if/else_if/else only in effect contexts. | The trigger database parses every nested clause as a trigger. Effect-only branch commands are not registered there and fail during startup parsing. |
| advisory | scope | country | Adding a philosophy debate variant group whose available_trigger only checks its own distinguishing condition, without excluding it from its base estate's (and any lower-priority sibling variant's) available_trigger | Every variant sharing a base_estate/base_group must exclude the base estate's available_trigger, and every later-id sibling variant's available_trigger, via variant_condition_lines()/emit_variant_exclusion() in scripts/philosophy_debate_codegen.py. Group id order (ascending) is the mutual-exclusion tie-break: lower id wins when two variants' raw conditions are both true. Never rely on tv_academy_debate_pick_unseated_*_group_effect's random_list weighting to resolve this. | Found while adding societal-value-driven variants: tv_academy_debate_pick_unseated_*_group_effect puts every group whose available_trigger is true into one equal-weight random_list, so a base estate whose available_trigger only checked has_estate/not-seated could still be picked over a variant even when the variant's condition (e.g. societal_value:land_vs_naval > 50) was clearly satisfied. |
| advisory | gui | country | Trying to bind a GUI row/list visible= expression to a scripted_trigger's result (e.g. an age-gate or has_advance check) so the display can filter a candidate list | GUI expressions cannot evaluate scripted triggers at all (see docs/knowledge/risk_cards/gui.md rule 2, which covers has_variable specifically — this generalizes to any scripted_trigger). Mirror the trigger's result into a country variable via a scripted_effect, and refresh that effect from a real lifecycle point (join/founding effect for immediate correctness, plus the feature's existing monthly_country_pulse hook for ongoing correctness) — never from a GUI/tooltip read path. Bind the GUI expression to the mirrored variable instead. | Discovered building the Academy of Sciences 'Potential Projects' card: the age-frontier eligibility trigger and has_advance are only readable in script scope, so the card needed a tv_research_open_age_ceiling variable (contiguous-prefix simplification of 4 age-gate triggers into one int) and per-advance tv_advance_<id>_researched variables, refreshed from the join effect and the existing tv_academy_of_sciences_monthly_pulse. Once mirrored, register the mirror variable with [[cmf_suppress_for_confirmed_dynamic_reference_variables]] — the GUI read does not count as a script-side use, so error.log still flags it as 'set but never used'. |
| advisory | localization | any | In a scripts_engineering_department/unique_wonder_ritual_content/<key>.py module's per-event localization dict, storing option-letter text directly alongside `"t"`/`"d"` keys (e.g. `{"t": ..., "d": ..., "a": ..., "d": "Install the Alms Prefect."}`) whenever an event has 4+ options and therefore reaches option letter `"d"`. | Nest per-event option text under its own `"options"` sub-dict (`{"t": ..., "d": ..., "options": {"a": ..., "d": ...}}`) and read `text["options"][letter]` in `build_localization`'s option loop. Never let the `for letter in ("a",...,"e")` option loop check `letter in text` directly against the same dict that holds the `"t"`/`"d"` keys. | Python dict literals silently keep only the last `"d"` key, so the event's real long-form description (e.g. ASSIGNMENT_EVENT_ID's 5-role summary) was discarded entirely and replaced by the 4th option's text ("Install the Alms Prefect.") before it ever reached the generator. Separately, the option loop's `if letter in text` matched the dict's own `"d"` desc key even on events with fewer than 4 real options (OPENING/SCANDAL/REWARD), re-emitting the description a second time under the `.d` key and producing the exact 'Duplicate localization key ... defined in both X and X' engine warning. bank_of_saint_george.py and dome_of_the_rock.py were unaffected because their option loops only check `("a","b","c")` and never reach letter `"d"`. |
| lint | modifier | any | Using a v1.2-era `_cost`/`_cost_modifier` static modifier name (e.g. `global_build_buildings_cost`, `stability_cost`, `court_spending_cost_modifier`, `global_bureaucracy_maintenance_cost_modifier`) — these were renamed to `_efficiency` in EU5 v1.3. | Rename to the v1.3 `_efficiency` identifier (see reference_game_files/game/main_menu/common/static_modifiers/00_modifier_types.txt) and negate the value: `color=bad->good` flips at the same time, so a value that reduced cost (e.g. -0.10) becomes a positive efficiency value (e.g. 0.10). Exception: `stability_cost` -> `stability_cost_efficiency` and `court_spending_cost_modifier` -> `court_spending_efficiency` (name doesn't just gain the suffix). Confirmed exact `x-1` negation via matched vanilla `inflation_impact`/`construction_wages` country_modifier blocks; for fields without a matched-block confirmation, this project's convention (see reverted scripts/archive/revert_1_3_modifier_efficiency_to_1_2_cost.ps1) is a uniform x-1 sign flip, treated as a placeholder pending a real balance pass. | v1.3 renamed ~29 `_cost`-suffixed static modifiers to `_efficiency`. Also lost the `min=-0.95` floor clamp on the three `global_bureaucracy_*_cost_modifier` -> `_efficiency` fields. Discovered during the v1.3 migration pass (2026-07-10); ~114 usage sites found across hand-written src files and ~10 generator data YAMLs (victory_paths.yaml, trade_league_laws.yaml, govhouse_laws.yaml, arts_exhibition_laws.yaml, academy_laws.yaml, wonder_final_buildings.yaml, wonder_generic_rituals.yaml, unique_wonders.yaml). Unrelated to the separate v1.3 building_type.cpp validator capping local_manpower/employment_size <= 0.0125 (undocumented, not a rename). |
| advisory | scope | country | Writing a subjugation-style effect (or peace/interaction/IO effect) that unconditionally calls `make_subject_of = { type = subject_type:vassal ... }` on a player-chosen overlord | `subject_type:vassal` is invisible/uncreatable for any overlord with `has_advance = samanta_advance` (Indian-subcontinent feudalism); its vanilla substitute is `subject_type:samanta`. Branch on `scope:<overlord> = { has_advance = samanta_advance }` and call `make_subject_of` with `subject_type:vassal` when absent, `subject_type:samanta` when present. See docs/technical/EU5_Modding_Knowledge_Base.md section 5.11. | Found fixing tv_alliance_sublime_empire_subjugate_member (src/in_game/common/generic_actions/tv_diplomatic_alliance_actions.txt), which called make_subject_of with a hardcoded subject_type:vassal — silently failing to subjugate alliance members whose leader country had researched samanta_advance. Any future TV mechanic that lets a player-chosen country become an overlord (alliance/IO subjugation, victory-path rewards, custom peace options) needs the same vassal/samanta branch instead of assuming vassal always works. |
| lint | event | any | `outcome = good` / `outcome = bad` at event top level (the `EAudioEventOutcome` audio-direction field) | Use `outcome = positive` / `outcome = negative` (keep `outcome = neutral` unchanged). Confirmed valid enum set from `reference_game_files/game/in_game/events/readme.txt` line 72: `outcome = positive/neutral/negative`, identical in both the pre-v1.3 and v1.3 reference commits — v1.3 only made the `pdx_persistent_reader.cpp` enum parser strict about rejecting values outside that set (error.log: `Failed to read enum! Type: "EAudioEventOutcome", Val "bad"/"good"`). | Found 2026-07-10 in error.log across ~841 sites in 17 TV event files after the v1.3 reference bump. Most sources were generator Python (hardcoded strings or dict literals in gen_tv_wonder_finalization_events.py, gen_tv_wonder_ownership_events.py, wonder_construction_event_lib.py's KIND_OUTCOMES, philosophy_debate_codegen.py's event_specs/notification_specs, and scripts_engineering_department/unique_wonder_ritual_content/*.py) or data/research_d_events.yaml, plus 6 hand-written event files with no generator (tv_academy_of_sciences_events.txt, tv_engineering_department_events.txt, tv_arts_exhibition_events.txt, tv_govhouse_events.txt, tv_medicine_events.txt, tv_research_events.txt). Fix at the generator/data source, never the generated .txt, then regenerate. |
| advisory | gui | country | Assuming a built-in country trigger (e.g. `num_of_advances_researched >= 50`) will show the player's current progress '(37/50)' in its custom_tooltip. Vanilla's own default tooltip loc for these triggers (see `NUM_ADVANCES_TRIGGER` in triggers_l_english.yml: "Has $COMPARATOR$ $NUM|V0$ [advances|E] researched") only substitutes the comparator and threshold — never the live value — and GetVariable-style GUI live-value display only works on country *variables*, not raw engine triggers/counts. | To show a live '(current/target)' readout for a raw trigger-only count, mirror it into a country variable via `set_variable = { name = tv_x_mirror value = <trigger_or_count> }` on the same monthly cadence that already checks the trigger (verify the count is usable as a `value =` expression first — e.g. `value = num_of_advances_researched` is confirmed vanilla usage in `reference_game_files/game/in_game/common/script_values/research_values.txt` and `estates/00_default.txt`). Then embed `[Country.MakeScope.GetVariable('tv_x_mirror').GetValue|0]` in the GUI-facing loc key only (e.g. an `_ESTABLISHMENT_STEP1_TEXT` card string), matching the existing `TV_CONQUEST_PROGRESS_DISPLAY`-style pattern. Do NOT also add this GUI `Country.MakeScope` macro to a sibling loc key that is rendered as a `custom_tooltip.text` inside a scripted_trigger — that renders in script-tooltip context (ROOT/THIS scopes), not GUI data-binding context, so the GUI-only accessor is unverified there and should be kept out. | Discovered while investigating a user report that the Academy of Sciences 'research at least 50 advances' requirement 'seemed unsatisfiable' after the EU5 1.3 patch. First fix attempt added a tv_science_advances_researched_mirror variable (refreshed monthly inside tv_io_establishment_mark_basic_requirements_effect) and appended the live value only to TV_SCIENCE_ESTABLISHMENT_STEP1_TEXT. The player then confirmed in-game that the mirrored value itself never moved — num_of_advances_researched is apparently stuck/broken under this EU5 1.3 build, not just missing a tooltip — so the mirror was reverted (see [[num_of_advances_researched_broken_eu5_1_3]] for the replacement stopgap). The GUI-vs-script-tooltip loc-key split documented above is still valid general guidance for any OTHER numeric establishment route that wants a live readout — conquest/prosperity/trade/diplomatic already track a working `var:tv_x_score` for their basic requirement, so the same live-display pattern could be extended to them the same way if requested. |
| advisory | scope | country | Gating a requirement on `num_of_advances_researched >= N` and assuming it will progress normally. As of the EU5 1.3 build tested 2026-07-10, this country trigger appeared stuck/non-updating in an actual playthrough (confirmed via the live tooltip added for TV_SCIENCE_ESTABLISHMENT_STEP1_TEXT — the displayed count never moved despite the player researching advances). Also, there is NO engine on_action that fires when a country finishes researching any advance — confirmed empty against `reference_official_defines/docs/on_actions.log` (no advance/research/tech/innovation entries) and by inspecting a live Steam Workshop mod (reference_mods/3668193813, 'National Destinies'): its DHE (dynamic_historical_event) reactions to specific advances use `monthly_chance = 100` + `trigger = { has_advance = <specific_advance> }` + `fire_only_once = yes` — i.e. the same has_advance-polling idiom, just applied per-known-advance, not a real completion callback. | Do not build a replacement counter that still reads num_of_advances_researched (for example by baseline-diffing it) — if the underlying value is stuck, the derived count is equally stuck. There is no Advance-completed on_action, so a reliable complete count requires polling an explicit candidate list with has_advance. Keep the expensive scan off monthly_country_pulse and isolate its generated branches in a dedicated file. Towards Victory's gen_tv_science_score_effects.py generates tv_science_score_effects.txt from every top-level definition under reference_game_files/game/in_game/common/advances/. The situation-start and yearly_country_pulse callers both require is_ai = no, so only player-controlled countries pay the scan cost. The generated effect must explicitly mark this as a temporary num_advance_researched / num_of_advances_researched engine workaround and should be removed when the upstream interface is confirmed fixed. The separate science-establishment basic gate remains waived until its gameplay requirement is explicitly restored. | Not independently reproduced against Paradox's own changelog/bug tracker — based on this project's playthrough observation on the tested 1.3 build. On 2026-07-24 the project adopted the complete but expensive yearly workaround for Scientific Victory progress, then isolated it for maintenance: gen_tv_science_score_effects.py extracts all top-level vanilla Advance ids and emits one has_advance branch per id into tv_science_score_effects.txt; the situation-start and tv_science_victory_yearly_pulse callers refresh player-controlled countries only. If a later patch fixes the engine count, restore direct num_of_advances_researched use, remove the independent scanner and yearly callback, and reconsider the separately waived science-establishment gate. See [[raw_trigger_count_has_no_live_tooltip_value]] for the reverted live-tooltip mirror attempt that surfaced this. |
| lint | modifier | location | auto_modifier with category = location (inside auto_modifiers/ file) | TRY_REPLACE entry in main_menu/common/static_modifiers/ with game_data.category = location | Location auto_modifiers are NON-FUNCTIONAL in EU5; bake effects into static_modifier values instead |
| needs_parser | scope | any | Using `save_temporary_scope_as = <name>` inside a `limit` block (or other trigger context) when the same effect/file also does a real `save_scope_as = <name>` with the identical name for the loop's payload, e.g. `every_in_list = { limit = { save_temporary_scope_as = tv_display_region ... } save_scope_as = tv_display_region ... }` | Give the trigger-context temporary save a distinct name (e.g. `tv_display_region_check`) and keep the real `save_scope_as = tv_display_region` for the effect body outside `limit`. This mod's convention (see `tv_govhouse_refresh_governor_display_effect`) is `<name>_check` for the limit-only temporary. | Engine logs `Trying to add the temporary target '<name>' which has the same name as a permanent target` and the effect/action silently fails at runtime (reproduced by appointing a regional governor). Temporary and permanent scope target names share one namespace per game-object scope; a `save_temporary_scope_as` registered inside a `limit`/trigger context conflicts with a later `save_scope_as` of the same name in the same effect chain, even though `save_temporary_scope_as` is otherwise the mandatory form inside trigger contexts (see EU5_Modding_Knowledge_Base.md 'save_scope_as is an effect only'). |
| lint | enum | location | location_rank:village | location_rank:rural_settlement | EU4 rank name; EU5 valid values are rural_settlement, town, city, megalopolis |
| lint | enum | location | location_rank:hamlet | location_rank:rural_settlement | EU4 rank name; EU5 valid values are rural_settlement, town, city, megalopolis |
| lint | enum | location | location_rank:metropolis | location_rank:megalopolis | EU4 rank name; EU5 valid values are rural_settlement, town, city, megalopolis |
| advisory | gui | any | Removing custom_tooltip from event options (wrong fix for dotted key confusion) | Keep custom_tooltip; verify key format via Step 2/3 — dotted suffix IS valid in event options | AI wrongly removed custom_tooltip thinking dotted key format was invalid. Never remove; only verify format. |
| advisory | event | any | Generating or referencing an event ID whose numeric part is 10000 or higher, such as tv_engineering_department.50011 | EU5 event IDs must be <namespace>.<integer> with the integer below 10000. Do high-cardinality dispatch before firing the event, or branch only over a small non-event-id dimension inside an already typed event. | The engine logs that five-digit numeric event IDs are invalid and can then report duplicate IDs as parser fallout. scripts/validate.py scans src/data/scripts for dotted event references whose numeric part is >= 10000. |
| lint | event | country | Putting `effect = { ... }` as a direct child of an event `option = { ... }` | Write effect calls directly in the option block, alongside `name` and optional `trigger` / `hidden_effect`. Use `hidden_effect = { ... }` only when the option chain should be hidden from the tooltip. | EU5 parses event option contents as an effect list. A direct child named effect is treated as an effect command named `effect`, which does not exist, producing 'Unknown effect effect'. |
| lint | script | scripted_effects | Defining a top-level scripted_trigger-style block inside common/scripted_effects | Generate or move the `_trigger = { ... }` block to common/scripted_triggers, and keep common/scripted_effects for real scripted effects only. | EU5 parses every top-level block in common/scripted_effects as a scripted effect. If a generated trigger body is placed there, trigger clauses such as has_variable, religious_unity, or any_international_organizations_member_of are parsed as effect commands and log `Unknown effect ...`. |
| lint | gui | any | text_single widget inside blockoverride "common_header_text" | blockoverride overrides a 'text' property value, not a widget container; do not add child widgets | Guessing block structure causes silent failures; always read cards.gui before using blockoverride |
| advisory | gui | any | Using GUI And(...) or Or(...) with three or more operands | Use And3(...) / Or3(...) for exactly three operands, or nest binary And(...) / Or(...) calls for larger expressions. | EU5 GUI And() and Or() are binary helpers, not variadic. And(a,b,c) fails conversion with 'Function And expected 2 arguments, got 3'. Pattern left empty because commas inside typed comparison calls make static regex detection noisy. |
| advisory | gui | texture | Putting modify_texture directly in a layout/type block such as a card_bg override that expands into a vbox | Put modify_texture inside a rendered background, icon, or other image-layer block. For card_bg overrides, preserve the card's background layers and place conditional texture modifiers inside the relevant background block. | modify_texture is handled by image-layer widgets, not layout containers. The ceremony-card generator emitted it beside using = bg_paper_card_situations in a card_bg block that the local card type expands into a vbox, causing 16 Property 'modify_texture' not handled messages and a failed ubervbox property setup. Context-sensitive GUI parsing is required to lint this safely. |
| advisory | gui | any | Using percent position directly on icon widgets for fixed-position overlays | For icon overlays on a fixed-size parent, use pixel positions; if percent placement is needed, put the icon inside a positioned widget wrapper instead. | Percent position is confirmed on vanilla widget wrappers, but percent position directly on these icon overlays collapsed them to the left edge. Fixed pixel bins are reliable for fixed-width bars. |
| advisory | gui | any | Putting ignoreinvisible on a plain widget/uberwidget | Use ignoreinvisible on layout containers such as hbox/vbox/scrollwidget content, not on a plain image or wrapper widget. To hide an image area, control the parent widget's visible expression and let the actual image children use their own visible expressions. | A plain widget logs Property 'ignoreinvisible' not handled and Error setting properties for the uberwidget. The attribute is accepted on layout containers elsewhere in the same panel, but not on the generic image wrapper. |
| advisory | gui | any | Building a conditional background-image column inside a plain widget/button with only expanding layout policies and no explicit image bounds | Give the clickable wrapper and image column fixed bounds, and size each visible background child with fixed pixels that match the content area. Keep the existing card/hbox/vbox structure instead of relying on invisible siblings or layoutpolicy alone to size the preview. | Conditional background widgets can collapse to the parent's left edge when the visible branch has no explicit size. In the wonder location panel, the plain widget/button wrapper also had to fill the parent before the image column, and the inner preview widgets needed fixed pixel sizes instead of percentage bounds. |
| advisory | gui | location | Using location.owner inside a scripted effect rendered by ShowScriptedEffectForScope with LocationView.GetLocation.MakeScope.Self | When the GUI passes the location itself as the effect scope, enter the owning country with owner ?= { ... }. Reserve location.owner for contexts that expose a separate location event target, such as building on_built. | In location-window tooltips the scripted effect root is already the location. A dotted location.owner chain is parsed as an event-target link named location, which is invalid from a location root and can also leave COUNTRY.GetName unavailable for add_country_modifier tooltip localization. |
| advisory | gui | any | Putting $LOCALIZATION_KEY$ substitutions inside raw_text GUI fields | Use a localized text = "KEY" field and put inline icons or static text in the localization value, or split dynamic raw text and localized text into separate widgets. | raw_text renders the string literally; $KEY$ substitutions are not expanded, so the UI displays the key. |
| advisory | script | location | Treating location prosperity reads as 0..100 percent points, such as dividing an average prosperity value by 100 before using it as a weight | Use the raw signed fractional prosperity value. For recovery priorities, negate the average directly and clamp below 0. | Vanilla checks use fractional thresholds such as prosperity < 1 and prosperity < -0.1; an extra divide by 100 makes prosperity-derived weights too small to compete. |
| advisory | script | international_organization | Trade League monopoly control computed from produced/traded/supply market proxies, old global-vs-IO goods totals, or representative-market-only samples | Compute monopoly control from a full scan of world markets using market every_export/every_import routes filtered by goods = goods:<good> and summed with trade_volume. Annual representative markets are display/decision support only. | produced_in_market:<good> and goods_demand_in_market(goods:<good>) are valid for weighting origin and consumer control and for choosing representative markets; traded_in_market and goods_supply_in_market are not route volume. Export/transit control uses market every_export trade_volume; consumer control uses market every_import trade_volume. |
| advisory | script | international_organization | Declaring or reading generated per-good Trade League monopoly/action state as IO variables | Store generated monopoly/action variables on the Trade League leader country; the country monthly pulse should enter the matching leader-owned IO for membership checks, then enter leader_country for state writes, and GUI should read GetInternationalOrganization.GetLeaderCountry.MakeScope.GetVariable(...). | The IO type should remain a light shell with leader/member behavior. Global annual marker variables remain global; per-IO/per-good state lives on the leader country. |
| advisory | script | international_organization | Running generated per-good Trade League monopoly maintenance from the Trade League IO type's monthly_effect | Leave the Trade League IO type without monthly_effect. Register a named monthly_country_pulse hook, save the current country scope, iterate every_international_organizations_member_of filtered to tv_trade_league and leader_country ?= the saved country, then call the IO-scoped monopoly refresh from inside that IO. | For this generated high-cardinality system, even hidden IO monthly maintenance was too expensive. A country pulse turns the work into a cheap country membership/leader check before the heavy IO refresh runs. |
| lint | on_action | country | Firing an event immediately from a condition path reached by country_monthly_pulse | Use trigger_event_* = { id = <event> days = 1 } for direct event fires, or put delay = { days = 1 } before event entries in native events/random_events blocks. | Monthly country pulse checks and event display should be separated by one day. scripts/validate.py walks monthly_country_pulse registry callbacks and TV helper calls to enforce this. |
| advisory | script | international_organization | Refreshing Trade League commercial-intelligence merchant power by repeatedly adding a temporary months=2 modifier, or adding permanent merchant power without first clearing the keyed state. | For each monthly intelligence refresh, clear only the tv_trade_intelligence_network merchant-power key from previously tracked markets, then reapply months = -1 merchant power for current Trade League members only when the current floor strength is at least 1. Also clear that same key when the Grand Merchant becomes invalid. | Commercial-intelligence strength is leader-country state, but merchant power is market/country keyed state. Permanent application must be synchronized by key so inactive decay, cancellation, death, and membership changes cannot leave stale power, while monopoly embargo keys remain independent. |
| lint | script | international_organization | Trade League starts with enacted laws or generated default policies | Keep Trade League lawless at creation: do not emit a laws block with policy mappings in the IO type, and do not mark generated Trade League policies as default. Diplomatic Alliance is a separate design exception that explicitly seeds baseline no-effect policies. | AI generated default level-1 Trade League policies into tv_trade_league's IO definition. This violated the Trade League design that policies are enacted later through votes. Vanilla setup shows an IO can explicitly start with laws = {  }; Diplomatic Alliance intentionally uses a non-empty laws block for its baseline tier-0 state. |
| advisory | script | international_organization | Defining a TV international organization without a matching io_opinion_<io_type> bias | Add src/in_game/common/biases/<io_type>_biases.txt with io_opinion_<io_type> = { value = <opinion> } and localize the same key. | EU5 applies automatic member opinions through io_opinion_<key>; missing the bias logs 'International Organization Type needs an opinion of other members'. |
| advisory | script | any | Passing a generic-action value selector or scoped variable directly as set_variable value, add_temporary_demand scale, or add_goods_supply amount | Wrap dynamic numeric reads in an explicit script-value block, e.g. value = { value = scope:target_1 }, value = { value = scope:target_1 divide = 2 }, scale = { value = scope:io.var:X }, or amount = { value = scope:io.var:X }. | Direct scoped numeric reads in these dynamic value slots can be interpreted as existence/truthy values and collapse to 1. The script-value block preserves the selected number and supports inline arithmetic. |
| advisory | script | any | Calling cleanup-only scripted effects from a visible generic action effect | Wrap cleanup/rebuild-only helper calls in hidden_effect = { ... }. scripts/validate.py enforces the registered high-risk helper list, including tv_governor_remove_effect. | Generic action effect tooltips can pre-evaluate cleanup helpers, causing variable/list cleanup chains to run through unsafe reads while merely hovering the button. Hidden output is required for these internal maintenance effects. |
| lint | modifier | country | trade_efficiency = <value> | Use trade_land_efficiency and trade_sea_efficiency separately (both category=country, percent) | trade_efficiency does not exist in EU5; split into trade_land_efficiency + trade_sea_efficiency |
| lint | localization | any | Smart/curly quotes (“”‘’) in localization YAML | Use straight ASCII double-quotes (") only; escape with \" inside values | PDX parser rejects non-ASCII quote characters; the failure is silent |
| lint | localization | any | A localization value contains a real physical newline inside the quoted string | Keep each localization key/value on one physical line. Generators should render intentional line breaks as \n inside the quoted value. | The localization reader treats the continuation line as a new key, causing invalid-character and missing-colon errors. scripts/validate.py checks main-menu localization physical lines. |
| needs_parser | localization | any | Defining the same localization key in more than one YAML file for the same language | Keep each localization key owned by exactly one file per language. When a generator takes over a key family, remove the stale manual copies instead of redefining them. | Main-menu localization YAML does not merge duplicate keys across files within one language pack. Startup logs a duplicate-key error and only one definition can win, so manual and generated files must have unambiguous ownership. |
| advisory | localization | international_organization | Custom IO law category localized only by raw category id (e.g. tv_science_factions) | Also define <CATEGORY_ID_UPPERCASE>_LAW_CATEGORY in main_menu localization, e.g. TV_SCIENCE_FACTIONS_LAW_CATEGORY | The vanilla IO Laws tab displays InternationalOrganizationLawCategory.GetName and looks up the uppercase *_LAW_CATEGORY key for law category headers; missing keys render literally. |
| advisory | gui | international_organization | Putting visible IO header controls such as action buttons in blockoverride "country_header_extra" on the standard organization header | Put compact IO header controls/readouts in ios_information_header_content_extra_1/2/3 with ios_header_content_extra_template, or override abilities_widget to include ios_information_header plus the controls. | In the standard organization_panel_default_header path, the visible lower header row is ios_information_header inside abilities_widget. country_header_extra is outside the character/header info row and can be absent or effectively hidden in UI bounds, so interactive controls may not render where expected. |
| lint | scope | location | add_province_modifier (EU4 effect) | add_location_modifier or add_static_modifier with location scope | EU4 province scope effects do not exist in EU5; use location-scoped equivalents |
| lint | scope | location | has_province_modifier (EU4 trigger) | has_location_modifier | EU4 province scope triggers do not exist in EU5 |
| lint | precision | any | float literal with 6+ decimal places (e.g. value = 0.123456789) | Round to at most 5 decimal places: value = 0.12346; EU5 engine reads no further | EU5 engine truncates all float literals at 5 decimal places; digits beyond the 5th are silently ignored |
| advisory | script | any | change_variable { divide = N } expecting float result when input < N (e.g. score ÷ large_threshold to get [0,1]) | Multiply by 100 (or desired precision) BEFORE dividing, then compare/cap at 100 instead of 1. For thresholds divisible by 100, use divide = (threshold÷100) in one step. | EU5 change_variable { divide } performs FLOOR integer division. E.g. 160÷2000=0, not 0.1. Use integer scale [0,100] with max=100 on GUI progressbar. Pattern left empty (no auto-detect) — all divide uses cannot be classified as bad without context. |
| advisory | gui | any | progressbar with dynamic GetVariable value binding and no explicit max | Always set max = 100 on the progressbar widget. Store values as integer [0,100] in scripted variables using integer-scale arithmetic. | Default progressbar max appears to be 1; GetVariable returning an integer 0-100 without matching max produces 0% for all values except 1 (which shows 100%). Pattern left empty — regex cannot reliably detect the missing-max + variable-value combination. |
| advisory | gui | any | Custom situation_panel leaves the shared default situation_subheader_content in place when no subheader content is needed | Add blockoverride "situation_subheader_content" {} near the top of the situation_panel block to suppress the default 45px empty row. | The shared situation_panel template includes a default subheader widget. Vanilla situation panels that do not use it override the block to empty; otherwise custom panels show unwanted blank space above their main content. |
| lint | gui | any | GetGoods('key'), GetGoodsFromKey, or MakeGoods('key') in GUI expressions to construct a Goods object from a string literal | Goods datacontext must come from a data model accessor chain (e.g. GoodsMarketEntry.GetGoods, Trade.GetGoods, GoodsItem.GetGoods in a foreach); there is no string-to-Goods lookup function in EU5 GUI | Exhaustive search of all reference_game_files *.gui files found zero occurrences of any string-to-Goods constructor; all goods access is typed accessor chains only |
| advisory | gui | any | Chaining .GetGoods from MakeScope.GetVariable(...) in GUI expressions | Use a real Goods datacontext from a typed data model/accessor, or generate static icon/name branches keyed by a saved numeric goods id. | Scope/numeric variables returned by MakeScope.GetVariable do not expose a .GetGoods accessor. Runtime error: Could not find data system function 'GetGoods'. |
