| advisory | gui | international_organization | Chaining .GetInternationalOrganization from MakeScope.GetVariable(...) in GUI expressions | Call OpenInternationalOrganizationView only with a typed InternationalOrganization object from a verified view/datamodel chain, such as InternationalOrganization.Self or OrgItem.GetOrg.Self. | Variables saved from script scope are not promoted to GUI InternationalOrganization objects by .GetInternationalOrganization. Runtime error: Could not find data system function 'GetInternationalOrganization'. |
| lint | gui | building | Passing a building key string to SelectLocationToBuildDefault / SelectLocationToBuildWithLocation in GUI | Pass a BuildingType object from GUI data context, such as BuildingItem.GetBuildingType, LocationBuildingItem.GetBuilding.GetType, or BuildingType.Self. If no BuildingType object is available in the panel, route the button through a generic action with a location select_trigger. | The vanilla build-location GUI calls SelectLocationToBuildDefault with a typed BuildingType object. A literal string key can leave the click appearing successful only because a following PlayAudioEffect runs, while no build-location interface opens. |
| lint | gui | any | datamodel = "[GoodsView.GetGoods]" inside a ContextualTooltipType or tooltipwidget template to iterate all goods and filter by key | GoodsView is a view-model object only available in goods_overview.gui panel scope; it is not accessible from tooltip/ContextualTooltipType contexts. No equivalent all-goods datamodel exists for tooltip scope. | Exhaustive search of reference_game_files found GoodsView only in the goods_overview panel; tooltip templates have no equivalent all-goods iteration datamodel |
| lint | scope | any | multiply_global_variable = { name = X  value = 0.95 } to scale a global variable in-place | Use set_global_variable with hardcoded per-case values: set_global_variable = { name = X  value = computed_literal }. For era-decay patterns, hardcode the result per age (idempotent, no dedup guard needed). If truly dynamic, compute in a local_variable then use set_global_variable = { name = X  value = local_var:tmp }. | Exhaustive search of reference_game_files/ and reference_mods/ found no multiply_global_variable effect. Only set_global_variable and change_global_variable exist for global-scope numeric variables. |
| lint | script | any | remove_from_variable_list = { name = X target = Y } for persistent variable lists | remove_list_variable = { name = X target = Y } to remove a specific entry. add_to_variable_list = { name = X target = Y } is still correct for adding. Temporary lists (add_to_list) use a different API: every_in_list = { list = X } and remove_from_list = X. | EU5 uses remove_list_variable (not remove_from_variable_list) to remove a scope reference from a persistent variable list. Confirmed by govhouse implementation. |
| advisory | gui | any | datacontext = "[InternationalOrganizationsView.GetPlayer]" inside action_button_diamond in an organization panel | Remove datacontext entirely. Use Player.MakeScope.GetVariable(...).IsSet in visible expressions instead of Country.MakeScope.GetVariable(...).IsSet | Adding datacontext to action_button_diamond overrides the widget's internal binding, causing tooltip generation to fail silently (empty tooltip, button stays grayed regardless of action potential). Vanilla IO panels never use datacontext on action_button_diamond. Use Player (global GUI binding) instead of Country (datacontext-dependent) to access player-country variables. |
| needs_parser | gui | any | A GUI button widget without tooltip/tooltipwidget, including visual button templates such as button_checkbox_auto or disabled button_regular_diamond | Give every button-derived widget its own tooltip or tooltipwidget, even if another transparent action_button overlays it or the button is disabled. | The engine validates the button widget itself. A separate overlay action_button with title/description does not satisfy tooltip validation for the underlying button_checkbox_auto, and disabled placeholder buttons still need a tooltip. |
| lint | scripted_values | any | Writing a leading + sign on positive float literals, e.g. value = +0.025 or change_variable = { ... value = +0.003 } | Omit the + sign entirely: value = 0.025. The EU5/Jomini parser does not accept a leading + on numeric literals and will misread the value. | Triggered by Python f-string format spec +:  e.g. f'{delta:+.5f}' emits +0.00357 for positive deltas. Fix: use f'{delta:.5f}' so no leading sign is emitted for positive values. |
| lint | gui | any | GetPlayerCountry.MakeScope.GetVariable(...) or GetPlayerCountry in situation panel GUI expressions | Use Country.MakeScope.GetVariable('name').GetValue — Country is the implicit player country datacontext in situation panels. For situation-scope variables use SituationView.GetActiveSituation.GetSituation.MakeScope.GetVariable('name') | Exhaustive search of all situation panel .gui files found zero occurrences of GetPlayerCountry; western_schism.gui:43 confirms Country.MakeScope as the correct chain for per-country variables |
| advisory | gui | any | Using > >= < <= == operators directly in GUI visible expressions to compare variable values (e.g. visible = "[Country.MakeScope.GetVariable('x').GetValue >= 3]") | Use typed comparison functions: GreaterThanOrEqualTo_CFixedPoint(Country.MakeScope.GetVariable('name').GetValue, '(CFixedPoint)N.0') for integer milestone variables; EqualTo_float(FixedPointToFloat(...GetValue), '(float)N.0') for float comparisons | EU5 GUI expression language has no inline comparison operators; all comparisons require named typed functions (GreaterThanOrEqualTo_CFixedPoint, LessThan_float, EqualTo_int32, etc.) with explicit type-cast literals |
| advisory | gui | any | Using an icon widget or designing custom icon syntax without first checking if @icon_name! inline texticon syntax already covers the case | Check font_icons.gui for existing texticon definitions first; use @icon_name! in raw_text/text/localization if available; fall back to icon widget; only design from scratch as last resort | EU5 has 180+ pre-defined inline icons via the @xxx! texticon system; skipping this check causes unnecessary widget complexity and misses the preferred engine-native approach |
| lint | event | any | Event ID with more than one dot, e.g. tv.conquest.1 = { type = country_event ... } | Event ID must be exactly <namespace>.<integer> with one dot. Use one namespace per category (e.g. namespace = tv_conquest, IDs tv_conquest.1..5), or flatten to sequential integers under one namespace. Localization keys for title/desc/option name CAN still use dotted suffixes (tv_conquest.1.t). | EU5 jomini_eventmanager splits the event ID on the FIRST dot. tv.conquest.1 becomes namespace 'tv' + integer 'conquest.1' which fails to parse, the event is dropped, and surviving entries collide as duplicates. Engine error: 'Failed to split namespaced event ID, needs to be in the form of namespace.integer'. |
| lint | gui | any | Setting parentanchor on a widget that is a direct child of an hbox or vbox (e.g. inside hbox = { widget = { parentanchor = vcenter ... } }) | Remove parentanchor from hbox/vbox children — the layout box arranges them automatically on the cross-axis. parentanchor remains valid on children of plain widget/window/container parents. | Brace-aware validator checks direct hbox/vbox child blocks for direct parentanchor properties. Children of progressbar/widget/window are unaffected. |
| lint | gui | any | Putting margin_top directly on a progressbar widget | Wrap the progressbar in a fixed-size widget/layout container, put vertical placement such as parentanchor/widgetanchor on the progressbar inside that wrapper, and leave margin_top off the progressbar itself. | EU5 progressbar does not handle margin_top. Text and container widgets may use margins, but progressbar vertical offset should be expressed through a parent wrapper. |
| advisory | gui | any | Setting size with a percentage component on a widget that is a direct child of an hbox or vbox, e.g. size = { 97% 72 } inside organization_custom_content | Remove the percentage size and rely on layout policies, or use non-percent fixed/min/max sizing when a fixed dimension is actually needed. For io_character_card in organization_custom_content, omit size/parentanchor and rely on the card type's built-in layoutpolicy_expanding. | Pattern is empty because static detection requires parsing GUI parent structure. Percent sizes are valid in some plain widget/fixedgrid contexts, but hbox/vbox children are arranged by the box layout and should be driven by layoutpolicy_* or stretch factors. |
| advisory | gui | any | Wrapping an organization_custom_content scrollarea in a child vbox and relying on autoresizescrollarea for dynamic overflow | Inject the scrollarea directly into organization_custom_content so it becomes a child of the vanilla inner vbox. Override margin_top/spacing on the block. Use a fixed viewport such as size = { 100% 430 } for bounded panels, or set the block layoutpolicy_vertical = expanding and the scrollarea using = layoutpolicy_expanding when the tab must claim the full IO pane from the common trailing expand spacer. Keep scrollwidget content with a natural/fixed vertical height (for example layoutpolicy_vertical = fixed plus ignoreinvisible = yes). Avoid autoresizescrollarea for tabs whose content can grow after state changes, because it can resize the viewport to the content and leave no useful overflow to scroll. | The vanilla custom content slot is already an inner vbox with margin_top = 5 followed by an expand spacer, and vanilla middle_kingdom injects children/properties directly into organization_custom_content. Adding another child vbox can make UI bounds look correct for the wrapper while the scrollarea sits lower inside it. A direct fixed viewport prevents autosize growth, but a full-pane custom tab also needs the injected block and scrollarea to be vertically expanding so the common trailing expand does not own the lower area. autoresizescrollarea is useful for dropdown-like natural-height widgets, but in this IO tab it grew with active exhibition content, removed overflow, and made the scrollarea stop scrolling. |
| advisory | gui | any | Using io_character_card outside a FilteredSortedList context without suppressing the inherited name sort highlights | Inside standalone io_character_card usages, add blockoverride "name_highlight" {} and blockoverride "character_entry_name_sort_by_highlight" {} unless the card is actually inside a sortable FilteredSortedList. | io_character_card uses character_entry, whose name highlight blocks call FilteredSortedList.IsKeyHoveredByWidgetName. Without a FilteredSortedList datacontext, the GUI data manager spams 'No context supplied' while the card is visible. |
| advisory | gui | any | A datamodel item block with more than one top-level child widget, e.g. item = { vbox = { ... } widget = { ... } } | Wrap all row content inside a single parent widget/vbox/hbox so the item block contains exactly one child. Put dividers or auxiliary widgets inside that parent. | GUI datamodel item descriptions can only contain a single child and no properties. Multiple top-level children log 'Malformed item desc' and can cause adjacent formatter noise while the list is rendered. |
| advisory | gui | any | RequirementsList with an empty blockoverride "block_title" { } to hide its title | Keep the nested title block and hide it explicitly: blockoverride "block_title" { block "block_title" { visible = no } } | An empty override removes RequirementsList's safe title replacement and exposes the underlying TooltipTableHeader textbox. Outside an actual tooltip, that header has no ExtraTooltipInfo context and continuously logs GetTintColor failures while the panel is hovered. |
| advisory | gui | any | Using TooltipTextBlock as a normal always-visible panel widget | Reserve TooltipTextBlock for real tooltip content (`tooltipwidget`, `ContextualTooltipType`, `AlertTooltipType`, etc.). In normal panels use text_single/text_multi or another non-tooltip text widget. | TooltipTextBlock inherits tooltip_text_block_template, whose font tint reads ExtraTooltipInfo.GetTintColor. Visible uses outside an actual tooltip context can continuously log FetchData failures while the panel is open. |
| advisory | gui | any | Wrapping tooltip rows that can grow in height in a fixed-size `widget` or an `expanding` child, e.g. `size = { W H }` around a `TooltipStringPairList` chain | Let the visible tooltip content establish its own height. Constrain width with `size = { W -1 }` or `minimumsize`, add `set_parent_size_to_minimum = yes` to the containing layout, and keep `ignoreinvisible = yes` so only visible rows contribute to the measured height. | A fixed-height tooltip row can keep the frame visually short while the modifier text spills below the border. The container must be height-auto if the modifier block can wrap to multiple lines. |
| lint | modifier | location | Direct numeric price in EU5 building definition, e.g. price = 200 (EU4 carry-over) | Use price = <named_constant> referencing a definition in common/prices/ (e.g. price = tv_academy_1_price with gold = 600). Capital buildings that scale with age use expensive = yes instead. | EU5 building gold cost uses named price constants from common/prices/ (e.g. price = expensive_estate_building at line 416 of estate_buildings.txt). Direct numeric literals like price = 200 are silently ignored. Named constants ARE valid. |
| lint | modifier | location | Boolean location modifier can_recruit_regiment_in_this_location assigned numeric 0/1 | Use can_recruit_regiment_in_this_location = yes or no. The modifier type is declared boolean=yes, and vanilla building modifiers use yes. | EU5 logs Malformed token near the numeric literal when a boolean modifier value is emitted as 1. Generated YAML data must preserve bools and render them as yes/no, not Python True/False or 1/0. |
| lint | modifier | wonder | Setting fixed-value wonder modifiers such as global_pop_conversion_speed, local_pop_assimilation_speed, local_manpower, or local_sailors above 0.5 | Keep these fixed-value entries at or below 0.5. If a percentage bonus was intended, use the matching *_modifier percent modifier instead. | EU5 multiplies these fixed-value modifier values by 1000 in game. scripts/validate.py checks wonder paths in src/ and data/ for direct YAML/script assignments to global_pop_assimilation_speed, global_pop_conversion_speed, local_pop_assimilation_speed, local_pop_conversion_speed, local_manpower, and local_sailors; percent *_modifier variants are intentionally excluded. |
| lint | script | any | Python-style boolean token True/False emitted into an EU5 common script | Render script booleans as yes or no. In Python generators, handle bool before int/float because bool is an int subclass. | YAML true/false loads as Python True/False; str(value) emits malformed tokens in Jomini scripts, as seen with global_peasants_migration_allowed = True. |
| advisory | encoding | any | Saving common/ scripts (static_modifiers, situations, scripted_effects, on_action, etc.) as plain UTF-8 without a byte-order mark | Save with UTF-8 BOM (first 3 bytes EF BB BF). In Python: write_bytes(b'\xef\xbb\xbf' + text.encode('utf-8')) or open(..., encoding='utf-8-sig'). Verify with `head -c 3 <file> | xxd` -> expect ef bb bf. | Engine retries without BOM but emits a warning per file per load. Localization YAMLs already require BOM; this rule extends the same requirement to common/ .txt scripts. Auto-generated files via scripts/gen_scaffold.py should always emit the BOM. |
| lint | localization | any | #Y[variable_expression]#! — color tag immediately followed by [ with no separator | Either omit the color tag and write [variable_expression] as plain text, OR add a space: #Y [variable_expression]#! — the space terminates the tag name so the parser reads #Y (tag) then ' value' (content). Note the leading space will be visible in the rendered string. | In EU5 Jomini localization, the color tag name is read from # until the first non-alphanumeric. When a [var] resolves to a number (e.g. 64) and is placed directly after #Y, the parser reads #Y64 as the tag name (unknown). The leading space in '#Y [var]' terminates the tag name at Y. |
| lint | localization | any | #YText#! / #Y{generated_text}#! — color tag immediately followed by normal text or generated text with no separator | Insert a separator after the color tag, e.g. #Y Text#!, #G Text#!, or #R Text#!. Do the same in generators before substituting localized names or concept text. | EU5/Jomini localization does not reliably treat #Y/#G/#R as complete color tags when text or a generated placeholder starts immediately after the tag. The formatter can treat the adjacent content as part of the formatting tag and drop or mangle the highlighted span. A visible space after the tag terminates it. |
| advisory | localization | any | custom_description { text = MY_LOC_KEY } where MY_LOC_KEY exists only in a localization YAML but not in common/trigger_localization/ | Create a trigger localization entry in src/in_game/common/trigger_localization/your_file.txt: MY_LOC_KEY = { global = MY_LOC_KEY }. The YAML key is still needed as the display text; the trigger_localization file is the bridge the engine uses at validation time. | EU5 engine validates custom_description text keys against common/trigger_localization/ at load. A YAML-only key passes validate.py but produces 'PostValidate of trigger custom_description returned false' and 'No trigger loc KEY' at game startup. |
| advisory | gui | any | Using HasVariable or is_set (script trigger syntax) inside a GUI visible expression to check whether a situation-scope variable exists | Use GetVariable('name').IsSet as the final accessor in a GUI visible expression: visible = "[SituationView.GetActiveSituation.GetSituation.MakeScope.GetVariable('name').IsSet]" | HasVariable is a script-side trigger and does not exist in the GUI expression language. GetVariable('name').IsSet is the GUI-native null guard for optional variables stored on situation/country scopes. |
| advisory | gui | any | Attempting to check equality of a CFixedPoint variable in a GUI visible expression without a typed function (e.g. assuming == works, or forgetting EqualTo_CFixedPoint exists) | Use EqualTo_CFixedPoint(expr, '(CFixedPoint)N.0') for equality; also available: LessThan_CFixedPoint, GreaterThan_CFixedPoint, LessThanOrEqualTo_CFixedPoint. Example: visible = "[EqualTo_CFixedPoint(Country.MakeScope.GetVariable('tv_best_path').GetValue, '(CFixedPoint)0.0')]" | EqualTo_CFixedPoint and related typed comparison functions are confirmed to exist in EU5 GUI. The inline == operator does not work in visible expressions. The _scripted_widgets.info file also documents EqualTo_CFixedPoint as the canonical equality check. |
| needs_parser | script | any | limit = { var:some_var >= var:other_var } when some_var may not be set on the scope | Add has_variable = some_var to the limit block before the var: comparison: limit = { has_variable = some_var  var:some_var >= var:other_var } | EU5 does NOT default unset variables to 0 in trigger comparisons; var:X on the left side of >= throws a script error and aborts the if-block. set_variable = { value = var:X } with an unset var appears to be silent (no error), so only limit comparisons need the has_variable guard. |
| needs_parser | event | country | Event option effect sets variable X and then immediately calls a visible helper that compares var:X, assuming the option tooltip pre-evaluator sees the new value | Wrap the state-changing/application sequence in hidden_effect, or make the called helper guard has_variable before direct var:X comparisons. For optional option triggers, use var:X ?= N. | EU5 event option tooltip generation may pre-evaluate option effects without committing earlier set_variable writes. A later visible direct var:X comparison can fetch an unset variable repeatedly while the event is open. |
| advisory | event | any | Putting global scans, high-cardinality dispatch, broadcasts, construction cleanup, map rebuilds, or other heavy work in an event option hidden_effect and expecting tooltip hover not to evaluate it | Keep option hidden_effect blocks to light guards or scheduler calls. Run heavy hidden work from a hidden = yes event immediate block or another non-tooltip execution path. | hidden_effect suppresses visible tooltip output, but it does not isolate event-option code from hover evaluation. The wonder finalization hidden chain was expensive because it included per-wonder/per-style dispatch, final building construction, cleanup, world-news broadcast, post-finalization priority caching, and project-state clearing. |
| advisory | event | any | Collapsing existing per-target event descriptions, target-specific options, per-id effect dispatch, staged GUI rows, or localized branch text into generic fallback behavior just to make a data-list change smaller | Preserve the existing player-facing structure when changing the data set. If the branch count is large, extend the appropriate generator/data source first, or stop and ask before changing structure. Do not silently replace target-specific UX with generic text/options or leave stale unreachable branches. | A data-list update is not permission to downgrade event/GUI structure. Removing or genericizing established branch structure is a design change and must be explicitly requested or approved. |
| needs_parser | event | any | Event option or visible event helper computes a temporary variable and a later visible helper compares var:X or uses scope.var:X as an effect value | Do not rely on temporary variables written earlier in the same visible option/effect chain. Use optional checks directly against persistent state, branch over bounded values with literals, or wrap the state-changing chain in hidden_effect when the nested tooltip is not needed. | The event option tooltip pre-evaluator did not commit tv_wonder_final_building_extra_levels before the nested helper ran. The same failure reappeared when Prosperity M1 reinitialized wonder module/helper buildings from partially built wonders, and again when unit-completion hover rebuilt helper/module levels through temporary current/target vars. For bounded 0..6 helper/module sync, emit literal per-level building deltas directly from persistent state instead of temporary combinable/current/target variables. For rounded countdown displays, prefer script-value operators such as ceiling/floor over scratch check variables like tv_wonder_remaining_months_floor_check. |
| needs_parser | event | variable_map | A visible event option/helper computes a temporary variable-map key and immediately uses global_variable_map(...|var:X) to choose a dynamic building for construct_building or change_building_level_in_location | Do not infer that dynamic building effects are unsupported. For tooltip-visible building effects, use a map key that already exists before the event opens, or branch over bounded non-wonder dimensions such as module part or ceremony style and use direct maps keyed by persistent project state. Capture the mapped building type into a local variable before switching to the location scope. | The failed chain was set_variable/change_variable of tv_wonder_module_building_key followed by set_local_variable value = global_variable_map(tv_wonder_module_key_to_building_type|var:tv_wonder_module_building_key) and building_type = local_var:tv_wonder_module_building_type. The working chain branches on tv_wonder_last_completed_part, captures from tv_wonder_id_to_<part>_module_building_type keyed by var:tv_wonder_locked, and then runs construct_building/change_building_level_in_location with local_var:tv_wonder_module_building_type. This preserves variable_map/numeric-id architecture without generated per-wonder dispatch tables. |
| advisory | event | building | Event option text manually states a building cost while the real construction is hidden behind pay_price plus free or silent construct_building | Put real building construction in the visible option effect chain when the player should see the cost. Let `construct_building` / `change_building_level_in_location` render the building name, price, and modifiers automatically. Use manual `pay_price` and hidden construction only for intentionally hidden or nonstandard funding flows. | The Governor's House local-administration event option said 'Build ... for 400 ducats' while a hidden follow-up paid tv_govhouse_local_administration_price and constructed the building with cost_multiplier = 0. Moving construct_building into the visible option made the engine render the actual building cost and avoids stale duplicated cost text. |
| advisory | event | variable_map | A non-silent confirmation event's hidden follow-up re-reads a variable_map/global_variable_map entry for the event's key and identity-matches it against the scope captured when the event was queued, instead of validating the captured scope directly | When an event can stay open for arbitrary game time before the player answers, do not treat a variable_map lookup at confirm-time as the source of truth for what the player is confirming. Validate the scope saved at queue time/immediate (ownership, building state, etc.) directly, and only write it back into the map after that direct validation succeeds. Make any trailing unconditional cleanup effect conditional on the confirmation having actually committed, so a failed confirm leaves the pending state for the real recovery/re-request path instead of silently discarding it. | tv_govhouse_confirm_local_administration_event_scope_effect re-derived scope:tv_govhouse_selected_admin_location from tv_govhouse_region_to_admin_location_map and required it to `this =` match scope:tv_govhouse_pending_local_admin_scope. Because tv_govhouse.5 is trigger_event_non_silently and can sit open while on_location_changed_owner / admin-location-loss handling legitimately reselects the map's candidate for the region, the map value could drift away from the location the player actually built on. The identity match then silently failed, tv_governor_appoint_effect (and therefore the tv_govhouse_region_display_list rebuild) was skipped, and the unconditional trailing cleanup discarded the pending request anyway -- making the region vanish from the Governor's House list. Fixed by validating scope:tv_govhouse_pending_local_admin_scope directly (owner ?= only -- NOT has_building_with_at_least_one_level, since the same instant construct_building in this option's visible effect does not reliably update that read yet) and gating the cleanup on a committed flag. |
| needs_parser | script | any | A visible generic action calls an effect that initializes persistent variables and then compares `var:X` later in the same visible effect chain, assuming action tooltip pre-evaluation commits the earlier writes | Hide action widgets until their prerequisite state exists, re-check prerequisites inside the action effect, and make reusable effects safe under pre-evaluation. For derived display/helper values, compute and compare `local_var:X` scratch values first, then write persistent display variables as outputs. For nullable persistent state, use `has_variable` / `var:X ?= ...` or branch from already-existing state instead of directly comparing variables set earlier in the same chain. | EU5 generic action card and tooltip rendering can pre-evaluate the action effect without committing preceding set_variable writes. A helper that initializes construction variables or Academy timeline display variables and then immediately reads them with direct var:X comparisons can spam Failed to fetch variable / Invalid left side errors while the card is merely visible, hovered, or clicked. Even a display refresh that sets a variable to 0 before comparing it needs optional reads or local scratch comparisons if it is reachable from a visible generic action. |
| advisory | script | any | A generic action effect wrapped in hidden_effect still initializes variables that a later helper compares during select_trigger hover pre-evaluation | Do not treat hidden_effect as a hover-evaluation barrier in generic actions. Guard the action effect on persistent state and make nested helper reads safe with optional variable links, persistent-state checks, or local scratch variables that are compared before persistent display variables are written. | Hovering the Governor appointment selector still reached tv_governor_apply_modifiers_effect even though the appointment chain used hidden_effect. The same class reappeared when the Science IO establishment action reached the Academy Philosophy timeline refresh through an effect chain. The pre-evaluator walked into helpers and needed safe reads or local scratch calculations for newly initialized task/display variables. |
| needs_parser | scope | generic_actions | A reusable helper reached from a generic-action effect compares IO leader/member state to `root`, assuming `root` is the action actor or current country | At helper entry or before changing scope, save the current owner/actor with `save_scope_as = <owner_scope>`, then compare against `scope:<owner_scope>` inside nested IO/member iterators. Use `root` only in contexts whose root scope is verified by the caller. | The Academy Philosophy debate progress helper used `leader_country ?= root` under `every_international_organizations_member_of`. Generic-action effect pre-evaluation reached it with no valid root RHS and logged `Invalid right side during comparison 'root'`; saving `tv_academy_debate_progress_owner` at helper start made the comparison stable. |
| advisory | scope | generic_actions | A scripted trigger reached from a generic-action effect uses `root`, `root.culture`, or another `root` link after entering a nested location/country scope, assuming the caller provides a valid country root. | At the scripted trigger's country-scope entry, use `save_temporary_scope_as = <owner_scope>` and use `scope:<owner_scope>` (or `scope:<owner_scope>.culture`) in nested trigger comparisons. Do not rely on the generic-action pre-evaluator to supply a valid `root`. In effect bodies, use `save_scope_as`; `save_temporary_scope_as` is for trigger contexts. | The Victory Path task generator emitted country-scoped candidate/current-condition triggers whose nested location checks compared `dominant_culture = root.culture` and `owner = root`. Generic-action effect-tooltip pre-evaluation supplied no valid root object, producing `Invalid right side during comparison 'culture'`, `Event target link 'dominant_culture' returned an invalid object`, and four `Invalid right side during comparison 'root'` errors. Each generated target trigger now saves `tv_victory_task_trigger_owner` with `save_temporary_scope_as` and uses that scope explicitly; candidate and display triggers use their own saved owners for every remaining nested country comparison. |
| advisory | scope | location | Using direct `owner = <scope>` or `dominant_culture = <scope>` comparisons while iterating all locations or a geography that can include non-ownable, water, or ownerless locations | Filter the iterator with `is_ownable = yes`, then use optional scope links: `dominant_culture ?= <culture>` and `NOT = { owner ?= <country> }`. Optional owner comparison safely treats an ownerless ownable location as not controlled; optional dominant-culture comparison ignores locations with no culture object. | Saving the country owner scope fixed the earlier invalid-root path but did not make each iterated location's nullable links valid. The task generator still scanned sea/non-ownable locations with direct `dominant_culture` and direct `owner` links, so Governor's House creation and its hover-pre-evaluation logged invalid-object errors repeatedly. The safe pattern is emitted for all culture and geography unification checks, including the culture-group display trigger. |
| advisory | localization | any | MY_KEY = { global = MY_KEY } when the trigger using MY_KEY has custom_tooltip nested inside custom_description | Declare all four perspectives: MY_KEY = { none = MY_KEY  global = MY_KEY  first = MY_KEY  third = MY_KEY }. Use this form for every trigger_localization entry as a safe default. | EU5 engine requires the 'first' perspective when custom_description wraps a nested custom_tooltip. A global-only entry causes '(BUG: KEY missing perspective. Could not find parameters for PROMOUN first Negative required false)' at runtime. Adding all four perspectives is zero-cost and prevents this for any trigger regardless of nesting. |
| lint | modifier | character | static_modifier or character_modifier using artist_skill_modifier = X | Use artist_skill_level_gain = X instead (already_percent = yes, category = character) | artist_skill_modifier does not exist. The correct modifier type for boosting artist skill development is artist_skill_level_gain (declared as already_percent in modifier_type_definitions). |
| lint | enum | country | government = monarchy (EU4 trigger style) | government_type = government_type:monarchy  (and similarly for republic, theocracy, tribe, steppe_horde) | EU5 government type trigger is government_type = government_type:X; the EU4 'government = X' form does not exist |
| lint | script | any | multiply_variable = { name = X value = Y } or divide_variable = { name = X value = Y } | change_variable = { name = X multiply = Y } or change_variable = { name = X divide = Y }. Both accept var:Z references: multiply = var:Z, divide = var:Z | multiply_variable and divide_variable do not exist in EU5; all variable math uses change_variable with multiply/divide parameters |
| lint | script | any | change_variable = { name = X value = 1 } | Use a named arithmetic operator with change_variable, e.g. change_variable = { name = X add = 1 }. If you want to overwrite the variable, use set_variable = { name = X value = 1 }. | change_variable does not accept a value field. The engine logs 'Failed to read value for change_variable' when value is used there. value belongs to set_variable; change_variable uses add/subtract/multiply/divide. False-positive edge case (confirmed live 2026-07 in scripts_engineering_department/unique_wonder_ritual_content/bank_of_saint_george.py): the single-line regex also fires on a legitimate nested scriptvalue block, e.g. `change_variable = { name = X add = { value = var:X multiply = var:Y } }` (compounding interest). That inline `add = { value = ... multiply = ... }` idiom is valid vanilla syntax -- confirmed at reference_game_files/game/in_game/events/disaster/turmoil_in_brandenburg.txt:1598 (`add_gold = { value = var:bra_owes_gold multiply = -1 }`) -- because the pattern's negated-character-class scan does not track brace depth and cannot tell the nested block's own `value =` from a top-level one on the same physical line. Do not silence this by weakening the regex (it still correctly catches the real mistake). Prefer rewriting the nested arithmetic as a named scratch variable instead: `set_variable = { name = tmp value = var:X }`, `change_variable = { name = tmp multiply = var:Y }`, `change_variable = { name = X add = var:tmp }`, `remove_variable = tmp` -- this also matches the codebase's existing scratch-variable convention (e.g. `*_ritual_total_favorable`, `*_combined_score`) better than a one-line nested scriptvalue. |
| lint | script | any | trigger_event = { id = X } or trigger_event = X inside scripted_effects or on_action | Use trigger_event_non_silently = { id = X } for popup events shown to the player, or trigger_event_silently = X for background events. Both accept simple form (= event_id) and object form (= { id = X days = Y }). | EU5 does not have a bare trigger_event effect. In scripted_effects and on_action files, the engine reports 'Unknown effect trigger_event'. The two valid variants are trigger_event_non_silently (shows popup) and trigger_event_silently (hidden/background). This distinction does not apply inside event option = { } blocks, which may use a different syntax. |
| advisory | event | country | Seeding a delayed trigger_event_silently recurring hidden-event loop without a persistent sentinel, active-prerequisite guard, or teardown cleanup | Seed the loop once from a lifecycle point, set a persistent sentinel, and call trigger_event_silently = { id = tv_feature.900 days = 1 }. In the hidden = yes country event's immediate block, re-check both the feature prerequisite and sentinel before doing work and before rescheduling itself. Clear the sentinel on teardown so already queued events stop naturally. | Delayed silent hidden events can implement daily immediate work outside monthly pulses and tooltip-rendered option chains, but every seed starts an independent loop. The sentinel prevents duplicate chains, and the teardown guard prevents a queued event from resurrecting a removed feature. |
| lint | script | any | every_in_variable_list = { name = X ... } or remove_from_variable_list = { name = X target = Y } for persistent variable lists | every_in_list = { variable = X ... } to iterate; remove_list_variable = { name = X target = Y } to remove a specific entry. add_to_variable_list = { name = X target = Y } is still correct for adding. Temporary lists (add_to_list) use a different API: every_in_list = { list = X } and remove_from_list = X. | EU5 has two distinct list types: (1) Temporary lists created with add_to_list — iterated with every_in_list { list = X }, removed with remove_from_list = X. (2) Persistent variable lists created with add_to_variable_list — iterated with every_in_list { variable = X }, removed with remove_list_variable { name = X target = Y }. every_in_variable_list and remove_from_variable_list do not exist in EU5; the engine reports 'Unknown effect' for both. |
| lint | script | country | add_treasury = X to add or subtract gold from a country | Use add_gold = X (simple literal: add_gold = -200) or add_gold = { value = script_value multiply = N } (computed form). Trigger side: gold >= 200 (literal integer) is valid. | add_treasury does not exist in EU5. The effect is add_gold; it accepts a bare literal integer (positive or negative) or a block with value/multiply. Error: 'Unknown effect add_treasury'. |
| advisory | script | country | Hardcoding add_gold = -N for an event option that should scale with the country's economy | Use vanilla scripted effect change_gold_effect = { scale = -N } for dynamic country-event costs. The effect scales with capital_wealth and country_economical_base and clamps the result. | Vanilla event laws.0005 uses change_gold_effect = { scale = -3.5 } for the paid +10 research-progress option. Use fixed add_gold only when a flat cost is the design intent. |
| advisory | script | any | Defining a price in common/prices/ without a corresponding modifier_type in common/modifier_type_definitions/ | For every price named {name} in common/prices/, a modifier type named {name}_cost_modifier MUST exist in common/modifier_type_definitions/. EU5's price_database.cpp validates this at startup and logs 'Missing modifier type for price. {name}_cost_modifier'. The modifier type allows future modifiers/events to discount the price. | This is an engine-enforced convention, not optional. Even if no modifier currently applies the discount, the modifier_type definition must be present. The naming is exact: price 'tv_academy_1_price' requires modifier type 'tv_academy_1_price_cost_modifier'. These appear unused but are implicitly consumed by the price loading system. |
| needs_parser | script | any | Defining a direct effect block on a vanilla hardcoded on_action hook inside a mod file when the same hook already exists in the vanilla _hardcoded on_action table | Extend hardcoded vanilla hooks with on_actions = { tv_named_callback } delegation and put the TV logic in the named callback's own effect block. Do not add a second direct effect block to the hardcoded hook itself. | Hardcoded on_action hooks are singleton by key for top-level effect blocks. A mod file that adds another direct effect to the same hardcoded hook will not merge the bodies; Jomini keeps the most recent effect and silently drops the earlier one. Use an on_actions delegate to route TV logic into a separate named on_action. |
| advisory | script | any | ordered_in_global_list limit block that references situation:X.var:Y without a prior has_variable = Y guard when Y might not be set | Wrap the entire ordered_in_global_list block in: if = { limit = { has_variable = Y } ordered_in_global_list = { ... } }. The outer if is evaluated once on the situation scope, so it is cheap. The inner limit still includes the var: reference but now only runs when Y is known to be set. | ordered_in_global_list evaluates its limit block for EVERY entry in the pool. If the referenced situation variable (e.g. tv_rank_1_country) was never set (because the pool was empty at situation on_start), every pool entry triggers a script error, producing N errors on first load. Guard with has_variable on the outer if to skip the whole pass when the prerequisite rank is absent. |
| advisory | script | any | Cascading ordered_in_global_list ranks that never clear old rank variables and gate later ranks only with has_variable = previous_rank | Treat ranking output as a fresh snapshot: remove all rank_N variables before rebuilding, ensure every pool entry has numeric order_by variables, and guard rank N with global_variable_list_size value > N-1 before running ordered_in_global_list. | A stale previous rank can survive from an earlier rebuild. If the current pool has fewer eligible entries, the later ordered_in_global_list pass can run with no selected item; prev is none, set_variable value = prev logs 'Value of wrong type ... none', and order_by/var reads may log failed variable fetches. Clearing outputs and checking current pool size prevents the pass from running when no current candidate exists. |
| lint | script | any | can_start = { game_is_initialized = yes } on a situation intended to spawn at game start | Use can_start = { always = yes } for situations with monthly_spawn_chance_unique that should exist from turn 1. game_is_initialized evaluates to current_date > 1337.5.1 (strictly greater); EU5 starts on exactly 1337.5.x, so the first spawn attempt fails and the situation spawns one month late. | game_is_initialized = { current_date > 1337.5.1 }. The strict > operator means month-1 (1337.5.x) evaluates to FALSE. Situation spawns in month 2 and becomes visible to the player in month 3 due to UI propagation delay. For non-popup situations with on_start initialization, always = yes is safe because monthly_spawn_chance_unique prevents duplicate instances. |
| advisory | scope | any | every_X = { root = { change_variable = { name = Y ... } } } where Y was set_variable'd on `this` before the loop, called from a situation's on_monthly | Use set_local_variable / change_local_variable for the accumulator. local_variable persists across all scope switches. Inside every_X, access the current-scope value directly (no prev. needed): change_local_variable = { name = acc add = some_value }. Then use local_var:acc after the loop. | In a situation's on_monthly, root = the situation scope, not the iterating country. set_variable stores onto `this` (country); root = { change_variable } then targets the situation — the accumulation goes to the wrong scope and the variable on the country stays 0. fix: local_variable is scope-independent and thread-local to the effect call. |
| lint | scope | any | target_flag = selected_artist (or any custom name) in select_trigger for 3-step generic actions | Use the vanilla naming convention: target_flag = target for step 1, target_flag = target_1 for step 2, target_flag = target_2 for step 3. Always wrap effect block in if = { limit = { exists = scope:target } ... } guard. | Custom target_flag names (e.g. selected_artist) in multi-step generic actions cause 'Undefined event target' / 'scope returned unset scope' errors at runtime. Engine appears to only reliably resolve target, target_1, target_2 for scope: lookups in effects. Missing exists guard causes THIRD_MOVE_CHARACTER_EFFECT loc failures when AI path skips selections. |
| needs_parser | scope | any | A later select_trigger uses source = world while visible/enabled/effect-preview logic reads scope:target or another earlier target_flag | Do not use source = world on a selector that depends on earlier selections. Omit source, use an inherited/actor source, or build an interaction_source_list so the chooser retains the previous target flags. | Trade League embargo selected a market, then opened a country selector with source = world and filtered countries by any_owned_location = { market = scope:target }. The selector showed no countries and logged 'Asking for a flag that's not in the interaction target chooser specified'. Removing source = world kept the later selector in the same interaction-target chooser so scope:target remained available. |
| advisory | generic_actions | any | A player-facing select_trigger relies on ai_interaction_source_list to populate its candidate list | Use interaction_source_list for candidates shared by player and AI. Reserve ai_interaction_source_list for AI-only overrides only when the player selector also has a valid source, source_flags, or interaction_source_list. | ai_interaction_source_list is only applied to AI countries. If it is the only custom source list, human players can see the selector's none_available_msg_key even when a valid target exists. |
| advisory | performance | generic_actions | A generic action allow block calls a has-any-valid-target trigger solely to prevent opening an empty select_trigger chooser | Do not duplicate selector candidate scans in allow. Keep allow for actor/state/resource prerequisites, put target eligibility in select_trigger visible/enabled, provide none_available_msg_key for an empty chooser, and keep the final effect guarded with exists = scope:target. | EU5 already handles a select_trigger with no selectable targets by showing the none_available_msg_key and blocking completion of that selection step. Repeating the same any-target scan in allow charges action rendering, tooltip evaluation, and AI/list evaluation before the chooser performs its own candidate pass. |
| advisory | scope | any | international_organization:type_key used in the same effect call that creates the IO via create_international_organization | Use save_scope_as = my_io inside create_international_organization = { ... }, then reference scope:my_io. Put all post-creation IO operations (every_country loops, set_variable) inside the creation block. Save the calling country BEFORE the block with save_scope_as = tv_founder; use scope:tv_founder inside loops to reference the founder. | international_organization:X link cannot resolve an IO created in the same effect invocation. Only works for IOs created in a previous tick or persisted from prior game state. Pattern empty — detection requires full effect-context analysis. |
| lint | script | any | change_variable = { name = X add = prev.var:some_var } — reading a named variable from prev scope in a numeric value position | Use set_local_variable / change_local_variable to capture the numeric value before the scope switch, then use add = local_var:name inside the new scope. Example: set_local_variable = { name = tmp value = var:some_var }  then in the other scope: change_variable = { name = X add = local_var:tmp }. | prev.var:X is valid only as a scope reference (country, religion, etc.), not as a numeric value source. Using it in an add = position causes 'Variable not of the value scope type. Type: empty'. Built-in scripted values like prev.num_locations are valid inline; only prev.var:X is broken. |
| lint | script | any | var:X ?= { this >= prev.var:some_numeric_var } or var:X > prev.var:some_numeric_var inside a trigger/effect limit | Use root.var:some_numeric_var when root is verified to be the original numeric owner, or capture the value before an effect iterator with set_local_variable and compare/use local_var:name inside the switched scope. | Using prev.var:X as the RHS of a numeric comparison can log 'Invalid right side during comparison 'var'' and make the condition fail every tick. In Engineering Department construction this caused the monthly material check to fail and kept the UI stuck on the materials-insufficient pause state. |
| needs_parser | script | any | local_var:X ?= var:Y, local_var:X > var:Y, or similar dynamic numeric comparisons inside an effect/key iterator when var:Y is owned by the outer scope | Capture the outer-scope value before entering the iterator with set_local_variable, then compare local_var to local_var inside the iterator. For equality, prefer NOT < and NOT > against the captured local instead of ?= var:Y. | During Engineering Department priority-candidate unregister, a variable-map key iterator compared a map-captured local candidate id against the country-scoped current candidate id with local_var:X ?= var:Y. EU5 logged 'Invalid right side during comparison var'. Capturing the country value into a second local before the iterator and comparing locals avoids the RHS parser path. |
| advisory | scope | location | Inside a location-scoped block (`capital = {}`, `var:site ?= {}`, `every_location_in_province = {}`, etc.), reading a country-owned numeric as plain `var:X` | Plain `var:X` always reads the current scope's variable store. After switching into a location/province block, use `root.var:X` when root is the original country owner, or capture the number before the scope switch with `set_local_variable` and use `local_var:X` inside the nested scope. | A nested location/province scope does not inherit country `var:` reads. If the numeric lives on the country, plain `var:X` inside the location block fetches the location variable store instead and can log 'Failed to fetch variable' / unset-scope errors. |
| advisory | scope | country | Country-scoped pulse, iterator, scripted trigger, or effect `limit` uses a direct `capital = { ... }` link, or puts sibling `exists = capital` before it expecting trigger short-circuiting | Use the optional link form `capital ?= { ... }` for trigger/limit checks. For effects, put `capital ?= { ... }` in the `limit`, and only enter direct `capital = { ... }` in the effect body after that `if` limit passes. | Some countries can have no valid capital object when yearly/monthly refresh effects run. Trigger blocks do not reliably short-circuit sibling lines, so `exists = capital` beside `capital = { ... }` still allows the direct link to be evaluated and can log `Event target link 'capital' returned an invalid object`. |
| lint | script | any | random = { chance = X random_list = { ... } } — nesting random_list as a child of random | random_list = { (100-X) = {} X = { random_list = { ... } } }. Use the outer random_list to implement the probability gate and the inner random_list to pick the specific outcome. Both levels of random_list are confirmed valid EU5 syntax. | random_list as a direct child of random = { chance = X } is silently dropped by the EU5 engine — the 8% roll fires but the random_list produces no outcome. All intended events are skipped with no error in the log. Fix: restructure to nested random_list where the high-weight option is the no-event case. |
| advisory | script | any | Applying a computed bonus to an IO typed variable via change_variable inside on_action (e.g. every_international_organizations_member_of { change_variable { name = tv_arts_intl_influence add = local_var:X } }) to implement a monthly character contribution, or moving an existing visible IO variable's monthly gain from monthly_change into monthly_country_pulse/country effects | Put the contribution in the IO type's monthly_change scripted value using leader_country.var:X chained access: if = { limit = { international_organization_has_leader = yes  leader_country = { has_variable = X } }  add = { desc = KEY  value = leader_country.var:X } }. Store computed helper values on the country via on_action only for cached inputs or GUI display. If a visible IO variable stops advancing, fix the monthly_change scope/conditions or its cached inputs; do not migrate the source arithmetic out of monthly_change. | IO typed variables (in the variables = {} block of the IO type) have a monthly_change scripted value that drives the tooltip and automatic monthly breakdown. Contributions applied externally via on_action or country pulse change_variable are invisible to that breakdown and can make the UI lie even when the number changes. The leader_country.var:X chain is valid in scripted value context (same family as root.var:X); it is NOT valid in change_variable { add = } effect context (see change_variable_add_prev_var anti-pattern). Set leader_type = country and call set_leader_country in on_creation or the creation block. |
| needs_parser | script | international_organization | A named positive monthly_change contribution wraps both signs in add = { desc = KEY ... } and conditionally flips opponents with an inner multiply = -1 | Put the stance condition outside the signed operation and emit mutually exclusive branches: supporters use if = { limit = { ... stance = support } add = { desc = KEY <positive magnitude> } }, while opponents use if = { limit = { ... stance = oppose } subtract = { desc = KEY <positive magnitude> } }. Neutral or inactive contributors should match neither branch. | IO monthly_change is also a scripted-value breakdown. Tooltip evaluation can expose a nested anonymous sign-flip operation even when its condition contributes nothing, producing phantom negative rows beside the named positive row and a 'missing tooltip' label. Keep each displayed signed contribution as its own desc-bearing add or subtract node; do not encode the sign with an unnamed nested multiply. |
| advisory | scope | any | international_organization:type_name or is_member_of_international_organization = type_name for an IO type without unique = yes | For non-unique IOs, never add unique = yes. Use on_creation = { set_leader_country = scope:actor } in the IO type definition to set the leader on creation. To scope to an IO instance at runtime, use every_international_organization = { limit = { type = type_name } ... }. Do NOT use international_organization:type_name or is_member_of_international_organization = type_name — both require unique = yes and will error at runtime for non-unique types. | tv_arts_exhibition is a non-unique IO: each country pursuing cultural victory creates their own independent instance. unique = yes is FORBIDDEN on this IO. The engine errors 'Failed to find a valid event target link' and 'does not lead to a unique international organization' for both the scope link and is_member_of_international_organization trigger. Pattern empty — static detection would require cross-referencing IO type definitions. |
| lint | scope | character | is_courtier = yes in every_character limit to find a character dispatched via move_country in a generic_action | Use is_alive = yes + has_variable = <dispatch_tag> instead. The dispatch tag (e.g. tv_on_exhibition) set before move_country is sufficient to uniquely identify the character. | move_country called unilaterally from a generic_action (owncountry type) does NOT grant is_courtier = yes in the destination court. Only formal country_interaction invitations (e.g. invite_artist where the recipient accepts) produce a true courtier relationship. The result: every_character { is_courtier = yes } silently skips the dispatched character in both yearly and monthly pulses — timer never increments, no events fire. |
| needs_parser | scope | any | set_local_variable inside var:X ?= {} and then reading that local_var from prev = {} or after leaving the dynamic scope. Example: var:tv_chief_artist ?= { set_local_variable = { name = ca value = artist_skill } change_local_variable = { name = ca multiply = 0.002 }  prev = { set_variable = { name = tv_chief_artist_monthly_bonus value = local_var:ca } } } | Use chained scope access from the original scope instead of switching when possible. To read a character skill stored in a country variable: value = var:tv_chief_artist.artist_skill. If a temporary value is needed inside a later scope, set_local_variable before entering that scope and read local_var:X inside it; that direction is valid. | The unreliable direction is dynamic-scope local_var escaping outward through prev or later outer-scope reads. The opposite direction is valid: set_local_variable before a var:X ?= {} / location scope switch, then use local_var:X inside the nested scope. |
| advisory | script | any | add_country_to_international_organization = prev or set_leader_country = prev placed as direct (visible) children of create_international_organization = { ... } | Wrap add_country_to_international_organization and set_leader_country inside hidden_effect = { } within the create_international_organization block: create_international_organization = { type = ... hidden_effect = { add_country_to_international_organization = prev  set_leader_country = prev } } | The vanilla tooltip strings THIRD_ADD_TO_INTERNATIONAL_ORGANIZATION_EFFECT and SET_LEADER_EFFECT use INTERNATIONAL_ORGANIZATION.GetName / .GetLeaderTitle. When these effects are visible children of create_international_organization, the engine evaluates the tooltip before the IO is fully instantiated, causing 'Promote INTERNATIONAL_ORGANIZATION returned nullptr' errors. Wrapping in hidden_effect suppresses tooltip generation and eliminates the errors. The outer create_international_organization tooltip (CREATE_INTERNATIONAL_ORGANIZATION_EFFECT) still shows correctly. |
| advisory | scope | any | var:X ?= { trigger_event_non_silently = ... } or var:X ?= { change_variable = ... } inside a random_list weight branch that is itself inside another random_list or every_character | Navigate to the scope ONCE before the random_list using save_scope_as: var:tv_exhibition_home_country ?= { save_scope_as = touring_home }. Then use scope:touring_home = { ... } inside every branch. Guard the outer random_list with if = { limit = { exists = scope:touring_home } }. | var:X ?= {} is the optional-navigation operator: it silently skips the block if the variable is unset or the target doesn't exist. Inside deeply nested random_list branches (every_character → random_list { 8 = { random_list { weight = { ... } } } }) the optional scope switch silently fails — no engine error is logged, the block is skipped, and no event fires. Pre-saving the target as a named scope before the random_list and using scope:name = {} inside branches is the reliable pattern. |
| lint | gui | any | visible = "[InternationalOrganizationsView.GetInternationalOrganization.HasLeader]" — HasLeader does not exist on InternationalOrganization in the GUI data system | Use IsLeaderCountryInPlace instead: visible = "[InternationalOrganizationsView.GetInternationalOrganization.IsLeaderCountryInPlace]". To display the IO leader character's name, use datacontext = "[InternationalOrganizationsView.GetInternationalOrganization.GetLeaderCountry.GetGovernment.GetRuler]" then text = "[Character.GetShortNameWithNoTooltip]". | HasLeader is a method on Unit (for army/navy leaders), not on InternationalOrganization. Calling it on an IO scope produces 'Could not find data system function HasLeader' and a malformed token error. IsLeaderCountryInPlace is the equivalent check for IOs. |
| lint | event | any | flavor = flavor_neutral / flavor_good / flavor_bad inside an event block | Use outcome = neutral / good / bad / positive / negative instead. Valid values confirmed from vanilla: neutral, good, bad, positive, negative. | EU5 uses outcome = (neutral|good|bad|positive|negative); there is no 'flavor' key on events. Using flavor causes 'Unexpected token: flavor' parse errors and the event will be missing an outcome. |
| advisory | event | any | Two or more top-level desc = { ... } blocks on the same event — e.g. desc = { desc = key_a trigger = {...} } followed by desc = { desc = key_b trigger = {...} } | Use a single desc block with first_valid + triggered_desc children: desc = { first_valid = { triggered_desc = { trigger = { ... } desc = key_a } triggered_desc = { trigger = { ... } desc = key_b } } } | EU5 allows only one top-level desc block per event. A second desc block at the same level causes 'Scoped pointer has already been read' and 'There should be only one special desc' engine errors. All conditional descriptions must be nested inside a single desc = { first_valid = { ... } } block. |
| needs_parser | scope | any | scope:actor = { has_variable = X } followed by scope:actor = { var:X = { ... } } in a generic action allow/custom_tooltip/trigger, expecting has_variable to suppress the var:X read | Use the optional variable link for variable-dependent reads: var:X ?= { ... }, var:X ?= 1, or var:X ?= { this >= N }. For generic actions, move a prerequisite to potential only when the action should disappear entirely. Do not rely on has_variable, custom_tooltip order, or separate sibling scope blocks to prevent a direct var:X read. | EU5 generic action UI evaluation may fetch every trigger line needed for tooltips, including sibling scope blocks inside allow. A direct var:X = { ... } fetches X before the logical result of has_variable can protect it, producing 'Failed to fetch variable' and 'Event target link var returned an unset scope'. Optional ?= returns false when the variable/scope is absent and avoids the error. |
| needs_parser | scope | generic_actions | OR = { NOT = { has_variable = X } var:X < N } in generic action potential/allow/select_trigger logic when X may be unset | Use optional variable links for nullable reads. For bounded counts, emit an OR of NOT has_variable plus var:X ?= each allowed literal value, or otherwise restructure so the value is guaranteed initialized before the action can render. Do not use direct var:X < N when X can be absent. | Generic action potential, allow, visible, and enabled blocks can be evaluated while startup/lifecycle variables are still absent. The engine still fetches the direct comparison inside an OR with NOT has_variable, logging Failed to fetch variable, unset var scope, and invalid left side errors. In Trade Chain add-node, the generated count < 20 checks were replaced with optional equality branches for 0..19 plus the unset branch. |
| advisory | gui | any | Binding an ordinary right-click generic action with right_click_and_hold_action, or omitting a separate right_action when the interaction requires an immediate right click | On the action_button, bind the separate action with right_action = { action_name = <id> }. Reserve right_click_and_hold_action for interactions that deliberately require the mouse button to be held. Give the right-click action its own potential, allow, effect, AI-list entry, message type, and localization. | The generic-action GUI contract distinguishes a single right click from a right-click-and-hold; choosing the hold field for an ordinary right click changes the player interaction instead of merely selecting another button. |
| advisory | gui | any | item = { widget = { datacontext = "[Scope.GetCharacter]" ... action_button_diamond = { parameter = { parameter_value = "[Character]" } } } } — rebinding datacontext to Character, then using [Character] in action_button_diamond parameter_value | MakeScope.GetList returns Scope wrappers. Do NOT rebind datacontext inside item's widget. Instead, use the full Scope.GetCharacter chain everywhere: raw_text = "[Scope.GetCharacter.GetNameToFit(...)]", parameter_value = "[Scope.GetCharacter]". action_button_diamond evaluates parameter_value in UIAction context, which does NOT inherit parent widget datacontext — so [Character] after datacontext rebind silently resolves to the outer context or null. | GetList always returns Scope wrappers regardless of stored type. action_button_diamond creates a UIAction object that evaluates parameter_value outside the widget's local datacontext chain. [Character] after datacontext = "[Scope.GetCharacter]" on the parent widget is NOT visible to the UIAction evaluator. Always use the explicit Scope.GetCharacter.xxx chain in every expression within the item, including parameter_value — never rely on datacontext inheritance through action_button_diamond. |
| advisory | modifier | any | INJECT:advance_name = { allow = { ... } } when the vanilla advance already has an allow block | Use REPLACE:advance_name = { <full vanilla block> allow = { OR = { <mod condition> <original condition> } } } to merge conditions. Use INJECT only for advances with no existing allow block. | EU5 advance definitions allow only ONE allow block. INJECT appends properties; a second allow block via INJECT is invalid. Must copy the full vanilla advance definition into a REPLACE block and merge the OR condition manually. |
| lint | scope | any | leader_country = scope:actor inside limit = {} inside any/every_international_organizations_member_of — non-optional = operator on leader_country | leader_country ?= scope:actor — use the optional scope operator so null leader_country returns false instead of erroring | EU5 tooltip evaluator does not short-circuit inside limit = {} blocks. any_international_organizations_member_of iterates ALL IOs the country is a member of (including vanilla coalitions, sects, unions) that have no leader_country. Using = on a null scope throws 'Event target link leader_country returned an invalid object'. Also: DA and Academy IOs had empty on_creation blocks that never called set_leader_country, making leader_country permanently null and breaking the leader block entirely. |
| lint | scope | country | limit = {} as a direct child of any_international_organizations_member_of in a scripted trigger | In trigger context, put iterator filters directly in the block: any_international_organizations_member_of = { international_organization_type = international_organization_type:<io_type> ... }. Reserve limit = {} for effect iterators such as every_international_organizations_member_of. | The trigger iterator treats direct children as trigger clauses; limit is not a trigger here and logs 'Unknown trigger type: limit'. |
| needs_parser | scope | country | Using any_international_organizations_member_of as an effect iterator in a scripted_effect body | Use every_international_organizations_member_of = { limit = { international_organization_type = international_organization_type:<io_type> ... } ... } when applying effects. Keep any_international_organizations_member_of only inside trigger contexts such as if/limit blocks. | any_international_organizations_member_of is trigger syntax. In effect context the engine logs Unknown effect any_international_organizations_member_of. |
| lint | scope | country | any_international_organizations_member_of directly under country_interaction potential | Wrap country-scoped checks in scope:actor = { ... } inside country_interaction potential. | country_interaction potential does not provide a country root for this iterator; direct usage logs inconsistent trigger scopes (invalid vs. country). Official defines document scope:actor as the acting country. |
| lint | script | any | Calling international_organization_chooses_new_leader anywhere in TV IO-related code (leader management actions, on_action, events, effects) | Never call international_organization_chooses_new_leader on TV IO types. The leader_country is locked to the founding country via on_creation and never reassigned. The great person character is tracked via independent country variables. This effect triggers the vanilla EU5 leader-election process, which violates the no-elections design. | international_organization_chooses_new_leader re-runs the leader selection process, which with leader_change_trigger_type = none still evaluates the leader = {} block and may reassign the engine-internal leader state in ways inconsistent with the design. The design deliberately omits this call to fully prevent any automatic leader change. |
| lint | scope | any | unique = yes on a TV IO type definition (tv_arts_exhibition, tv_diplomatic_alliance, tv_academy_of_sciences) | All three TV IOs are non-unique (unique = no). Never add unique = yes. Use every_international_organizations_member_of = { limit = { international_organization_type = international_organization_type:<io_type> } ... } to scope to them from country scope. | Adding unique = yes would collapse per-country IO instances into one shared instance, breaking the independent-per-country design. It also makes international_organization:type_name syntax appear to work, masking the underlying design violation. |
| lint | scope | any | international_organization:tv_arts_exhibition (or tv_diplomatic_alliance, tv_academy_of_sciences) scope link | Use every_international_organizations_member_of = { limit = { international_organization_type = international_organization_type:<io_type> } ... }. The international_organization:type_name syntax requires unique = yes and produces a runtime error for non-unique types. | All three TV IO types have unique = no. Using international_organization:X on non-unique types causes 'does not lead to a unique international organization' and the scope resolves to null, silently skipping all effects. |
| lint | scope | any | appointed_leader.adm, appointed_leader.dip, appointed_leader.artist_skill, etc. in TV IO monthly_change or monthly_effect blocks | Use leader_country.var:tv_xxx_leader_char.attribute instead. Example: value = leader_country.var:tv_academy_leader_char.adm. Always guard with international_organization_has_leader = yes and leader_country = { has_variable = tv_xxx_leader_char }. | appointed_leader accesses the leader via the vanilla IO leader mechanism, which in our design is decoupled from the great person character. The great person is stored as a country variable on leader_country; chained access leader_country.var:tv_xxx_leader_char.attribute is the correct pattern. |
| lint | modifier | character | value = character.artist_skill  multiply = 0.002 (assuming artist_skill is an integer 0-100 scale) | Use multiply = 0.1 (or other factor scaled to the 0-1 range). artist_skill in EU5 is a normalized float 0.0-1.0, not a raw integer. A skill-5 artist returns ~0.5, not 5. | artist_skill is a level-based attribute normalized to [0,1] by the engine when accessed as a scripted value. Design formulas written assuming 0-100 scale must divide the intended multiplier by 100. |
| advisory | modifier | character | Static modifier used with add_character_modifier but defined without game_data = { category = character }, e.g. my_modifier = { } | All static modifiers intended for use with add_character_modifier / has_character_modifier MUST include game_data = { category = character }. Example: my_modifier = { game_data = { category = character } [effects...] } | Without category = character, the engine does not recognize the modifier as character-scoped. add_character_modifier runs (tooltip shows the grant) but the modifier is not stored on the character — has_character_modifier returns false and no title prefix fires. Confirmed at runtime: tv_chief_artist_role_modifier = {} appeared in appoint tooltip but never on the character panel. |
| lint | modifier | country | Applying Governor's House War Tent tactics with add_character_modifier | Governor's House War Tent tactics intentionally use country-scoped modifiers as a fallback because the combat modifiers did not take effect from the general character in this system. Keep a code comment near the country modifier application explaining the approximation. | Vanilla character static modifiers can carry commander/army combat modifiers in general, but the Governor's House stored-general tactic application did not affect combat from the character. This feature is intentionally approximated as a country modifier. |
| lint | gui | character | Arts leader select_trigger using column.data = character_info | Use column.data = character_artist for artist-only character selectors so the list uses the artist ability view. | The character_artist attribute column swaps the character list's ability area and tooltip to artist skill. Generic character_info shows ruler-style ADM/DIP/MIL information and is the wrong view for selecting a Chief Artist. |
| advisory | gui | scripted_triggers | custom_description { text = KEY  has_variable = X  var:X >= N  has_variable = Y  var:Y >= M } — multiple independent conditions collapsed into one block | Use one custom_tooltip block per condition group at the top level of the scripted_trigger body (implicit AND). The extra_trigger_block YAML field must be a complete custom_tooltip { } block, not raw condition lines. | When multiple sub-conditions are collapsed into one custom_description or custom_tooltip, the player sees a single pass/fail indicator for all conditions combined. Separate custom_tooltip blocks each show their own pass/fail state, allowing the player to see which specific sub-condition is not yet met. |
| advisory | localization | character | Defining a second character_title_prefix block in a separate customizable_localization file | Copy/generate the full vanilla character_title.txt and insert custom title text entries into the original character_title_prefix block. | Top-level customizable localization keys are unique database entries, not additive merge blocks. A duplicate block is ignored, so custom character title prefixes never load. |
| advisory | gui | international_organization | Relying on InternationalOrganization.GetIcon for a mod-defined IO without providing a matching icon texture | Add a DDS at main_menu/gfx/interface/icons/international_organizations/<io_type>.dds. The filename should match the international_organization type key, e.g. tv_arts_exhibition.dds. | The shared IO UI calls InternationalOrganization.GetIcon. Vanilla IO icon files are named after IO type keys in gfx/interface/icons/international_organizations/. Mod IOs without matching files fall back to the generic IO icon. |
| advisory | localization | game_concepts | Adding localization keys game_concept_tv_x / game_concept_tv_x_desc and then using [tv_x|e] without defining tv_x in main_menu/common/game_concepts | Register every custom concept ID in src/main_menu/common/game_concepts/tv_game_concepts.txt with at least a texture block. Localization alone is not enough. | Unregistered custom concepts in square brackets are parsed as data-system functions. The engine logs 'Could not find data system function <concept>' and renders ERROR:<concept> in tooltip text. |
| advisory | gui | localization | Using dynamic concept-link helpers with key expressions that can be localized before the concept-link parser consumes them | Feed SelectGameConcept or [...|E] only registered raw concept ids. For generated dynamic routes, store a numeric id and build ids such as tv_wonder_display_<id>; use Localize(Concatenate('game_concept_', key)) only for intentionally plain text. | The failing test used a flag-derived wonder key that localized before link parsing. The bug is the localized key source, not numeric-id dynamic concept routing. |
| advisory | gui | variable_map | Using GetFlagName from a script variable or variable_map value as a raw script key for localization, modifier, texture, or effect-name concatenation | If the downstream GUI needs a stable script key, store a numeric id and generate static id branches, or use a typed datamodel object with a real GetKey accessor. Treat GetFlagName as display/localization-prone text. | Concept/localized flags can return their localized display name in GUI. Concatenating that result produced game_concept_法罗斯灯塔 and 法罗斯灯塔_level_3 instead of raw script keys. |
| needs_parser | scope | variable_map | Using a quoted variable_map/global_variable_map/local_variable_map lookup directly as an equality right-hand side, trigger comparison RHS, or effect parameter after scope switching | Use variable-map lookups primarily as left-side scope links. When the mapped value is needed as a RHS, first capture it with set_local_variable or set_variable in the map-owning scope, then use local_var:X or var:X. Prefer local_var for temporary values. | Quoted variable-map expressions are reliable for entering the mapped value scope on the left side. Runtime tests found direct RHS use unreliable for value extraction or comparisons, e.g. building_type = "variable_map(...)" inside a later location/building scope. Cache the mapped value before entering the later scope. |
| advisory | performance | variable_map | Calling a variable-map/global-map rebuild effect, including an if-needed rebuild router, from GUI refresh, country cache refresh, tooltip/projection, selection, monthly read, or other hot/read paths as a compatibility fallback | Build global variable-map indexes only at lifecycle points: startup, save-load initialization, data-change regeneration, or explicit initialization. If a read misses a map, fix the current lifecycle hook or the call order that reads before initialization; do not add if-needed routers, old-schema repair, or legacy state preservation. | Engineering Department briefly added tv_wonder_index_rebuild_global_maps_if_needed_effect with a map-version gate, then called it from cache/module refresh paths. That violated the no-compatibility policy, hid lifecycle/call-order bugs, and charged hot paths for defensive global-map checks. |
| needs_parser | scope | variable_map | Using root inside a variable-map key callback called from a generic-action effect to write back to the action actor/current country | Save the current owner scope before random_key_in_variable_map/every_key_in_variable_map, then write back through scope:<saved_owner>; preserve the variable-map key stream instead of replacing it with generated per-id branches. | Generic action selector hover can pre-evaluate action effects without a usable root event target. A proposal deck helper using random_key_in_variable_map -> root = { set_variable = { value = prev } } logged Event target link 'root' returned an unset scope and left no proposal slots. |
| needs_parser | scope | variable_map | Calling is_key_in_variable_map on the current every_key_in_variable_map/ordered_key_in_variable_map callback scope, especially with target = this | Save the map-owning scope before the key iterator, copy this into a local variable, and run is_key_in_variable_map inside scope:<saved_owner> with target = local_var:<key>. | In a key iterator callback the current scope may be the numeric key itself (for example 1.00), which does not support scope-owned variables. Membership checks for sibling country-scoped maps must execute from the saved country/map owner while the key is passed through a local variable. |
| needs_parser | scope | variable_map | Calling is_key_in_variable_map or variable_map(...) for a country-scoped map directly from an every_in_list/random_in_list/any_in_list item scope such as a region, location, or character | Save the country/map owner before the item iterator, copy the dynamic key to a local variable, and run both is_key_in_variable_map and the quoted variable_map scope link inside scope:<saved_owner>. | Governor's House display refresh iterated tv_govhouse_region_list, making the current scope a region. The country-scoped region_to_admin_location map check then logged 'This scope doesn't support variables' for each region.
